# ASCII Ocean

> A canvas ocean with sharks, koi, dolphins, orcas and humpbacks: click to feed them, each with its own feeding moves, in natural colours with depth and a water surface.

- Kind: component
- Docs: https://www.huanngdev.site/components/ascii-ocean
- Registry: https://www.huanngdev.site/r/ascii-ocean.json
- Apply: copy each file to the same path from the repository root. Leave the bytes unchanged.

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/math.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

export const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => (v < lo ? lo : v > hi ? hi : v);

/** Uniform random source, returning values in [0, 1). */
export type Rng = () => number;

/** Seeded 32-bit PRNG (mulberry32): the same seed always yields the same sequence. */
export function createRng(seed: number): Rng {
  let s = seed >>> 0;
  return () => {
    s = (s + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = s;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export const rand = (rng: Rng, lo: number, hi: number) => lo + rng() * (hi - lo);

export const randInt = (rng: Rng, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.floor(rand(rng, lo, hi + 1));

export function smoothstep(e0: number, e1: number, x: number) {
  const t = clamp((x - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

/** Shortest signed angle from a to b, in (-PI, PI]. */
export function angleDiff(a: number, b: number) {
  let d = (b - a) % TAU;
  if (d > Math.PI) d -= TAU;
  if (d < -Math.PI) d += TAU;
  return d;
}

/** Cheap integer hash → [0, 1). Deterministic, so textures stay stable. */
export function hash3(x: number, y: number, z: number) {
  let h = Math.imul(x | 0, 374761393) ^ Math.imul(y | 0, 668265263) ^ Math.imul(z | 0, 1442695041);
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
  h ^= h >>> 16;
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

const K1 = 1 / 260;
const K2 = 1 / 310;
const K3 = 1 / 190;
const CURRENT_SCALE = 3400;

/**
 * Divergence-free ocean current: the curl of a slowly drifting stream
 * function ψ(x, y, t). Because it has no sources or sinks, it moves the
 * schools around without bunching them up in one corner.
 */
export function current(x: number, y: number, t: number, out: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const a = x * K1 + t * 0.07;
  const b = y * K2 - t * 0.05;
  const c = (x + y) * K3 + t * 0.11;
  // u = ∂ψ/∂y, v = -∂ψ/∂x with ψ = sin(a)cos(b) + 0.5 sin(c)
  out.x = (-K2 * Math.sin(a) * Math.sin(b) + 0.5 * K3 * Math.cos(c)) * CURRENT_SCALE;
  out.y = (-K1 * Math.cos(a) * Math.cos(b) - 0.5 * K3 * Math.cos(c)) * CURRENT_SCALE;
  return out;
}

/** Converts OKLCH (L 0–1, C, H in degrees) to sRGB bytes 0–255, clamped to the gamut. */
export function oklchToRgb(l: number, c: number, h: number): [number, number, number] {
  const rad = (h * Math.PI) / 180;
  const a = c * Math.cos(rad);
  const b = c * Math.sin(rad);
  // OKLab → LMS (cube-rooted), then cube to LMS.
  const l_ = l + 0.3963377774 * a + 0.2158037573 * b;
  const m_ = l - 0.1055613458 * a - 0.0638541728 * b;
  const s_ = l - 0.0894841775 * a - 1.291485548 * b;
  const L3 = l_ * l_ * l_;
  const M3 = m_ * m_ * m_;
  const S3 = s_ * s_ * s_;
  // LMS → linear sRGB.
  const lin = [
    4.0767416621 * L3 - 3.3077115913 * M3 + 0.2309699292 * S3,
    -1.2684380046 * L3 + 2.6097574011 * M3 - 0.3413193965 * S3,
    -0.0041960863 * L3 - 0.7034186147 * M3 + 1.707614701 * S3,
  ];
  // Linear → gamma-encoded sRGB, then bytes.
  const encode = (x: number) =>
    Math.round(
      clamp((x <= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 * x : 1.055 * Math.pow(x, 1 / 2.4) - 0.055) * 255, 0, 255),
    );
  return [encode(lin[0]), encode(lin[1]), encode(lin[2])];
}

/** OKLab lightness (0–1) of an sRGB colour given as bytes 0–255: the inverse of the lightness path in oklchToRgb. */
export function rgbToOklabL(r: number, g: number, b: number): number {
  const lin = (c: number) => {
    const x = c / 255;
    return x <= 0.04045 ? x / 12.92 : Math.pow((x + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4);
  };
  const R = lin(r);
  const G = lin(g);
  const B = lin(b);
  // Linear sRGB → LMS, cube root, then the lightness row of the OKLab matrix.
  const l = Math.cbrt(0.4122214708 * R + 0.5363325363 * G + 0.0514459929 * B);
  const m = Math.cbrt(0.2119034982 * R + 0.6806995451 * G + 0.1073969566 * B);
  const s = Math.cbrt(0.0883024619 * R + 0.2817188376 * G + 0.6299787005 * B);
  return 0.2104542553 * l + 0.793617785 * m - 0.0040720468 * s;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/palette.ts
```ts
import { DEFAULT_PALETTE, type OceanPalette } from "./renderer";

/**
 * Returns a reader that takes the page colours from an element's `background-color` and `color`.
 * It keeps one probe canvas, created on first use, to resolve any CSS colour (including oklch()).
 */
export function createPaletteReader(): (el: HTMLElement) => OceanPalette {
  let probe: CanvasRenderingContext2D | null = null;

  /** Resolves any CSS colour (including oklch()) to rgba bytes via a reusable 1×1 canvas. */
  const toRgba = (css: string): Uint8ClampedArray | null => {
    if (!probe) {
      const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
      canvas.width = 1;
      canvas.height = 1;
      probe = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    }
    const ctx = probe;
    if (!ctx) return null;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
    ctx.fillStyle = "#000";
    ctx.fillStyle = css;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 1, 1);
    return ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 1, 1).data;
  };

  return (el: HTMLElement): OceanPalette => {
    const style = getComputedStyle(el);
    const bg = toRgba(style.backgroundColor);
    const fg = toRgba(style.color);
    // No background colour (transparent): keep the default look.
    if (!bg || !fg || bg[3] === 0) return DEFAULT_PALETTE;
    const luminance = (0.2126 * bg[0] + 0.7152 * bg[1] + 0.0722 * bg[2]) / 255;
    return {
      background: [bg[0], bg[1], bg[2]],
      foreground: [fg[0], fg[1], fg[2]],
      mode: luminance > 0.5 ? "light" : "dark",
    };
  };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/species.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
import type { Rng } from "./math";

export interface Species {
  name: string;
  cruise: number;
  maxSpeed: number;
  minSpeed: number;
  /** Relative odds of a new school being this species. */
  weight: number;
  schoolSize: [number, number];
  /** Personal space in grid cells (x, y), so every fish gets its own glyph. */
  spacing: [number, number];
  // Behaviour weights. Different mixes produce different collective shapes
  // (Couzin et al. 2002): strong alignment gives polarised schools and
  // ribbons, low alignment gives swarms, and a tangential pull gives a mill.
  align: number;
  cohesion: number;
  jitter: number;
  /** Tangential pull around the local centre: >0 makes a rotating bait ball. */
  mill: number;
  /** Damps vertical speed; real fish mostly cruise level. */
  verticalDrag: number;
}

export const SPECIES: Species[] = [
  {
    name: "sardine",
    cruise: 36,
    maxSpeed: 64,
    minSpeed: 18,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [18, 28],
    spacing: [1.8, 1.1],
    align: 1.6,
    cohesion: 1.6,
    jitter: 40,
    mill: 70,
    verticalDrag: 0.5,
  },
  {
    name: "anchovy",
    cruise: 46,
    maxSpeed: 78,
    minSpeed: 22,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [20, 32],
    spacing: [1.5, 1],
    align: 3.2,
    cohesion: 0.45,
    jitter: 30,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 1.4,
  },
  {
    name: "silverside",
    cruise: 40,
    maxSpeed: 70,
    minSpeed: 20,
    weight: 3,
    schoolSize: [12, 20],
    spacing: [2, 1.1],
    align: 2.4,
    cohesion: 1.1,
    jitter: 35,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 1.6,
  },
  {
    name: "mackerel",
    cruise: 32,
    maxSpeed: 60,
    minSpeed: 16,
    weight: 2,
    schoolSize: [5, 9],
    spacing: [2.6, 1.3],
    align: 2,
    cohesion: 0.9,
    jitter: 25,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 2,
  },
  {
    name: "goby",
    cruise: 26,
    maxSpeed: 56,
    minSpeed: 10,
    weight: 1,
    schoolSize: [8, 12],
    spacing: [1.4, 1.1],
    align: 0.35,
    cohesion: 1.4,
    jitter: 110,
    mill: 0,
    verticalDrag: 0.3,
  },
];

const TOTAL_WEIGHT = SPECIES.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.weight, 0);

export function pickSpecies(rng: Rng) {
  let r = rng() * TOTAL_WEIGHT;
  for (let i = 0; i < SPECIES.length; i++) {
    r -= SPECIES[i].weight;
    if (r <= 0) return i;
  }
  return 0;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/types.ts
```ts
import type { SharkKind } from "../world";

/** Every animal the sim and renderer know how to draw. Later rounds extend this union. */
export type CreatureSpecies = SharkKind | "koi" | "bottlenose" | "orca" | "humpback";

/** Which animals to put in the water. `size` is an extra multiplier on the real-size scaling. */
export type CreatureGroup = {
  species: CreatureSpecies;
  /** How many of this species, 1–12. Default 1. */
  count?: number;
  /** Extra size multiplier on top of the species' real-size scaling. Default 1. */
  size?: number;
  /** Coat to use, or a list to pick from per animal (koi: kohaku, sanke, showa, tancho, ogon, asagi). Ignored by species without coats. */
  variety?: string | string[];
};

/** `ocean` is the open sea; `pond` is a bait-free tank, so baitFish defaults to off. */
export type OceanScene = "ocean" | "pond";

/** `stylized` compresses the size gap between species; `true` keeps real length ratios. */
export type OceanScale = "stylized" | "true";

/** What a pointer press does to the water. */
export type OceanInteraction =
  /** A press drops food: nearby animals swim over to eat, each in its own way. */
  | "feed"
  /** A press flashes the water. */
  | "splash"
  /** Presses are ignored. */
  | "none";

/** OKLCH colour: lightness 0–1, chroma, hue in degrees. */
export type Oklch = [number, number, number];

/** Koi colour varieties; each one is a coat on the koi species. */
export type KoiVariety = "kohaku" | "sanke" | "showa" | "tancho" | "ogon" | "asagi";

/** Where on the body a point is, for painting: s along (0 snout → 1 tail), v across in px (signed), L the body length, across 0 (spine) → 1 (edge of the body), flag the renderer's cell flag (body, fin, ridge, eye...). */
export type PaintPoint = {
  s: number;
  v: number;
  L: number;
  across: number;
  flag: number;
  id: number;
  /** The animal has rolled far enough to show its underside (orca belly, a shark's pale ventral side). */
  belly: boolean;
  /** How far the underside shows, 0 (none) → 1 (fully rolled), eased across the roll so a belly grows in. `belly` is `bellyAmount > 0.5`. */
  bellyAmount: number;
};

/** Natural colours: `regions[0]` is the base skin; `paint` returns a region index for a point. */
export interface Coat {
  regions: readonly Oklch[];
  paint?: (p: PaintPoint) => number;
  /** Sparkle the scale glyphs in pale gold and white instead of the accent colours (ogon). */
  glint?: boolean;
}

/** `natural` paints animals in their real colours on any theme; `theme` keeps them greyscale. */
export type OceanColors = "natural" | "theme";

/** Tail drawn instead of the shark's forked caudal fin. */
export type TailShape =
  /** Whale/dolphin flukes: a horizontal, swept-back wing. From `1 - length` to the tail tip; the tips reach the end, the centre notch sits `notch` before it. */
  | { kind: "fluke"; span: number; length: number; notch: number }
  /** A fish's vertical caudal fin seen from above: a narrow blade behind the peduncle. `width` is its half-width at the tip at rest, `twist` the extra half-width at the peak of a beat, both fractions of length. */
  | { kind: "caudal"; length: number; width: number; twist: number };

/** Body outline and fin layout per species. All values are fractions of length. */
export interface BodyShape {
  halfWidth: (s: number) => number;
  /** Pectoral fin: leading root, trailing root, tip position along the body, and reach. */
  pectoral: [number, number, number, number];
  /** Notched (falcate) trailing edge on the pectorals. */
  falcate: boolean;
  /** Rounded paddle pectorals (koi, orca) instead of the triangle; `falcate` is ignored. */
  paddle?: boolean;
  /** Pelvic fins: leading root, trailing root, tip, reach. */
  pelvic: [number, number, number, number];
  /** Forked caudal fin reach, or 0 when the tail is a ribbon (zebra shark). */
  caudal: number;
  dorsal: [number, number];
  secondDorsal: [number, number];
  /**
   * Hammerheads' cephalofoil: half-span, how far its tips sweep back, its depth, and the
   * notch in the middle of the front edge. All fractions of length; absent for other sharks.
   */
  head?: { span: number; sweep: number; depth: number; notch: number };
  /** null when the eyes sit somewhere else (hammerhead: on the cephalofoil tips). */
  eye: [number, number] | null;
  gills: [number, number];
  /** Skin colour multipliers (r, g, b). */
  tint: [number, number, number];
  /** Tail drawn instead of the shark's forked caudal fin. */
  tail?: TailShape;
  /** Multiplies the side-to-side body wave. Default 1. Whales and dolphins beat their tails up and down, so from above they barely bend; use about 0.15 for them. */
  undulation?: number;
  /** Pectoral flex amplitude. Default 0.06. */
  finFlap?: number;
  /** Pectoral flex frequency as a multiple of the tail-beat phase. Default 0.5. */
  finFlapRate?: number;
}

export interface CreatureDef {
  /** Typical adult length in metres. Sets the size ratios between species. */
  realLength: number;
  shape: BodyShape;
  /** Dark skin markings at body point (s along, v across); `id` seeds per-animal patterns. */
  marking?: (s: number, v: number, L: number, across: number, id: number) => boolean;
  /** Pale ridges along the back (zebra shark). */
  paleRidge?: (s: number, across: number) => boolean;
  /** Named natural colour schemes (koi varieties). Sharks leave it out and keep their tinted greys. */
  coats?: Record<string, Coat>;
  /** Coat used when a group gives no variety. */
  defaultCoat?: string;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/paint.ts
```ts
import { hash3 } from "../math";

/**
 * Smooth value noise in [0, 1): bilinear blend of hashed lattice values, eased with smoothstep
 * so the pattern has no visible grid. Deterministic for a given (x, y, seed).
 */
export function valueNoise(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
  const x0 = Math.floor(x);
  const y0 = Math.floor(y);
  const tx = x - x0;
  const ty = y - y0;
  const u = tx * tx * (3 - 2 * tx);
  const v = ty * ty * (3 - 2 * ty);
  const a = hash3(x0, y0, seed);
  const b = hash3(x0 + 1, y0, seed);
  const c = hash3(x0, y0 + 1, seed);
  const d = hash3(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, seed);
  return a + (b - a) * u + (c - a) * v + (a - b - c + d) * u * v;
}

/**
 * Body-anchored patch noise: `s` along the body, `vOverL` across it as a fraction of the length,
 * `scale` the patch size, and `salt` picks an independent pattern. Patches ride along with the skin.
 */
export function blobs(s: number, vOverL: number, id: number, scale: number, salt: number): number {
  return valueNoise(s / scale, vOverL / scale, id * 131 + salt);
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/sharks.ts
```ts
import { hash3, smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { SharkKind } from "../world";
import type { CreatureDef } from "./types";

/** Classic top-down half-width at s ∈ [0, 1] (snout → tail), as a fraction of length. */
function classicHalfWidth(s: number) {
  if (s < 0.13) return 0.09 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.13) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.95);
  if (s < 0.3) return 0.09 + 0.016 * smoothstep(0.13, 0.3, s);
  if (s < 0.84) return 0.019 + 0.087 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.3) / 0.54, 1.25);
  const k = (s - 0.84) / 0.16;
  return 0.019 * (1 - k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
}

/** Shark body outlines, fin layouts and skin, per species. */
export const SHARKS: Record<SharkKind, CreatureDef> = {
  classic: {
    realLength: 5,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: classicHalfWidth,
      pectoral: [0.15, 0.43, 0.55, 0.26],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.57, 0.65, 0.69, 0.085],
      caudal: 0.085,
      dorsal: [0.33, 0.47],
      secondDorsal: [0.71, 0.76],
      eye: [0.1, 0.14],
      gills: [0.19, 0.29],
      tint: [0.92, 0.97, 1.05],
    },
  },
  // Slim neck under a wide, swept-back cephalofoil (drawn in sampleBody),
  // tall first dorsal, long upper tail lobe. Bronze-olive grey.
  hammerhead: {
    realLength: 3.5,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.06) return 0.045;
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.045 + 0.061 * smoothstep(0.06, 0.3, s);
        return classicHalfWidth(s) * 0.92;
      },
      head: { span: 0.17, sweep: 0.035, depth: 0.09, notch: 0 },
      pectoral: [0.17, 0.4, 0.5, 0.22],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.56, 0.63, 0.67, 0.075],
      caudal: 0.11,
      dorsal: [0.28, 0.44],
      secondDorsal: [0.7, 0.74],
      eye: null,
      gills: [0.16, 0.26],
      tint: [0.96, 0.98, 0.9],
    },
  },
  // Blunt, square snout on a heavy body; barred back. Warm grey-brown.
  tiger: {
    realLength: 3.8,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.13) return 0.1 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.13) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.45);
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.1 + 0.014 * smoothstep(0.13, 0.3, s);
        if (s < 0.84) return 0.019 + 0.095 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.3) / 0.54, 1.2);
        const k = (s - 0.84) / 0.16;
        return 0.019 * (1 - k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      pectoral: [0.15, 0.42, 0.53, 0.25],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.57, 0.65, 0.69, 0.085],
      caudal: 0.095,
      dorsal: [0.33, 0.46],
      secondDorsal: [0.71, 0.75],
      eye: [0.08, 0.12],
      gills: [0.19, 0.28],
      tint: [1.02, 0.95, 0.84],
    },
    marking: (s, v, L, across) => {
      // Wavy vertical bars over the back half, fading out toward the head.
      if (s < 0.2 || s > 0.9 || across > 0.85) return false;
      const wobble = 0.35 * Math.sin((v / L) * 25 + s * 5);
      const bar = Math.sin(s * Math.PI * 2 * 9 + wobble);
      return bar > 0.65 - 0.5 * smoothstep(0.2, 0.42, s) + 0.4 * smoothstep(0.8, 0.9, s);
    },
  },
  // Short rounded head, body done by half-length, then a long ribbon of a
  // tail fin. Broad rounded pectorals, spotted sandy skin.
  zebra: {
    realLength: 2.3,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.1) return 0.075 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.1) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.7);
        if (s < 0.28) return 0.075 + 0.025 * smoothstep(0.1, 0.28, s);
        if (s < 0.55) return 0.022 + 0.078 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.28) / 0.27, 1.1);
        const k = (s - 0.55) / 0.45;
        return 0.022 * (1 - k * k) + 0.012 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      pectoral: [0.12, 0.3, 0.36, 0.19],
      falcate: false,
      pelvic: [0.42, 0.48, 0.51, 0.065],
      caudal: 0,
      dorsal: [0.36, 0.44],
      secondDorsal: [0.49, 0.53],
      eye: [0.06, 0.09],
      gills: [0.13, 0.2],
      tint: [1.08, 0.97, 0.74],
    },
    marking: (s, v, L, _across, id) => {
      // Leopard spots on a jittered grid, so they ride along with the skin.
      if (s < 0.07) return false;
      const gu = (s * L) / (L * 0.06);
      const gv = v / (L * 0.045);
      const cu = Math.floor(gu);
      const cv = Math.floor(gv);
      const jx = hash3(cu, cv, id) * 0.5 + 0.25;
      const jy = hash3(cu, cv, id + 11) * 0.5 + 0.25;
      return (gu - cu - jx) ** 2 + (gv - cv - jy) ** 2 < 0.08;
    },
    // Pale ridges down the back, between the spots.
    paleRidge: (s, across) =>
      s > 0.08 && s < 0.55 && (across < 0.1 || Math.abs(across - 0.6) < 0.08),
  },
  // Great hammerhead: a broader, nearly straight-edged cephalofoil with a notch at the centre,
  // a towering sickle first dorsal (drawn long and bright from above), and a cooler slate skin.
  greathammer: {
    realLength: 5,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.05) return 0.04;
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.04 + 0.07 * smoothstep(0.05, 0.3, s);
        return classicHalfWidth(s) * 0.95;
      },
      head: { span: 0.21, sweep: 0.006, depth: 0.085, notch: 0.014 },
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.38, 0.49, 0.24],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.55, 0.62, 0.67, 0.085],
      caudal: 0.13,
      dorsal: [0.24, 0.45],
      secondDorsal: [0.68, 0.74],
      eye: null,
      gills: [0.15, 0.25],
      tint: [0.86, 0.93, 1.02],
    },
  },
  // Shortfin mako: a long conical snout, a torpedo body that narrows hard to the tail stalk,
  // and a tall crescent tail. Metallic indigo back.
  mako: {
    realLength: 3.5,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.17) return 0.088 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.17) * Math.PI) / 2), 1.5);
        if (s < 0.32) return 0.088 + 0.012 * smoothstep(0.17, 0.32, s);
        if (s < 0.86) return 0.014 + 0.086 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.32) / 0.54, 1.45);
        const k = (s - 0.86) / 0.14;
        return 0.014 * (1 - k) + 0.01 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      pectoral: [0.18, 0.4, 0.5, 0.21],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.58, 0.64, 0.68, 0.06],
      caudal: 0.13,
      dorsal: [0.34, 0.45],
      secondDorsal: [0.76, 0.79],
      eye: [0.11, 0.15],
      gills: [0.2, 0.3],
      tint: [0.78, 0.9, 1.2],
    },
  },
  // Thresher: a compact body over the front half, then the upper tail lobe as a long whip
  // that's as long as the shark itself. Big eyes, long sickle pectorals, bronze-violet skin.
  thresher: {
    realLength: 5,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.08) return 0.07 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.08) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.75);
        if (s < 0.2) return 0.07 + 0.02 * smoothstep(0.08, 0.2, s);
        if (s < 0.48) return 0.012 + 0.078 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.2) / 0.28, 1.3);
        // The whip: thin, with a slight sickle swell before it tapers out.
        const k = (s - 0.48) / 0.52;
        return 0.012 * (1 - k) + 0.006 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI * 0.9) + 0.002;
      },
      pectoral: [0.12, 0.27, 0.36, 0.2],
      falcate: true,
      pelvic: [0.37, 0.41, 0.44, 0.045],
      caudal: 0,
      dorsal: [0.24, 0.32],
      secondDorsal: [0.45, 0.47],
      eye: [0.05, 0.1],
      gills: [0.11, 0.18],
      tint: [1.0, 0.9, 1.08],
    },
  },
  // Nurse shark: a blunt, broad head, a heavy flattened body with both dorsals set far back,
  // rounded paddle pectorals, and a long low tail with no lower lobe. Tawny brown.
  nurse: {
    realLength: 2.8,
    shape: {
      halfWidth: (s) => {
        if (s < 0.08) return 0.085 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.08) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.4);
        if (s < 0.3) return 0.085 + 0.02 * smoothstep(0.08, 0.3, s);
        if (s < 0.72) return 0.022 + 0.083 * Math.pow(1 - (s - 0.3) / 0.42, 1.1);
        const k = (s - 0.72) / 0.28;
        return 0.022 * (1 - k * k) + 0.014 * Math.sin(k * Math.PI);
      },
      pectoral: [0.16, 0.34, 0.4, 0.17],
      falcate: false,
      pelvic: [0.45, 0.52, 0.55, 0.075],
      caudal: 0,
      dorsal: [0.5, 0.58],
      secondDorsal: [0.63, 0.69],
      eye: [0.05, 0.075],
      gills: [0.14, 0.22],
      tint: [1.1, 0.96, 0.76],
    },
  },
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/koi.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import { blobs } from "./paint";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, KoiVariety, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const SHIROJI: Oklch = [0.95, 0.015, 90];
const HI: Oklch = [0.6, 0.19, 35];
const SUMI: Oklch = [0.22, 0.01, 260];
const YAMABUKI: Oklch = [0.8, 0.15, 85];
const ASAGI: Oklch = [0.6, 0.045, 240];
const ASAGI_NET: Oklch = [0.74, 0.03, 235];

const fract = (x: number) => x - Math.floor(x);

/** Kohaku's red patches on white; a red cap on the head for two in three fish. Shared with sanke. */
function kohakuRed(p: PaintPoint) {
  if (p.s < 0.03 || p.s > 0.86) return false;
  const cap = p.s < 0.14 && p.id % 3 !== 0;
  return blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.09, 1) > (cap ? 0.35 : 0.5);
}

/** Realistic koi colourings, from the species notes. Region 0 is always the base skin. */
const coats: Record<KoiVariety, Coat> = {
  // White with red patches; white fins.
  kohaku: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI],
    paint: (p) => (p.flag !== FLAG_FIN && kohakuRed(p) ? 1 : 0),
  },
  // Kohaku with small black spots on the white, none on the head.
  sanke: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI, SUMI],
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
      if (kohakuRed(p)) return 1;
      if (
        p.s >= 0.15 &&
        p.s <= 0.8 &&
        p.across < 0.85 &&
        blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.035, 7) > 0.8
      ) {
        return 2;
      }
      return 0;
    },
  },
  // Black base with red and white over it; red wins where they overlap. Black reaches the head.
  // Pectoral roots (motoguro) are black; the other fins are white.
  showa: {
    regions: [SUMI, HI, SHIROJI],
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.s < 0.4 && p.across < 0.35 ? 0 : 2;
      // Thresholds tuned so black stays the commonest colour (the noise centres on 0.5).
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.1, 3) > 0.62) return 1;
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.08, 5) > (p.s < 0.12 ? 0.8 : 0.66)) return 2;
      return 0;
    },
  },
  // White with one round red spot on the head, centred at 0.075 L and radius 0.045 L.
  tancho: {
    regions: [SHIROJI, HI],
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
      const dx = (p.s - 0.075) * p.L;
      const r = 0.045 * p.L;
      return dx * dx + p.v * p.v < r * r ? 1 : 0;
    },
  },
  // A single metallic gold colour that catches the light.
  ogon: {
    regions: [YAMABUKI],
    glint: true,
    paint: () => 0,
  },
  // Blue-grey back with a lighter scale net; red flanks and pectoral roots; a pale plain head.
  asagi: {
    regions: [ASAGI, ASAGI_NET, HI],
    paint: (p) => {
      if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return p.s < 0.4 && p.across < 0.35 ? 2 : 1;
      if (p.s < 0.14) return 1;
      if (p.across > 0.62) return 2;
      // Scale net: lines on a grid of cells 0.035 L square.
      return fract(p.s / 0.035) < 0.2 || fract(p.v / p.L / 0.035) < 0.2 ? 1 : 0;
    },
  },
};

/** Thick, deep-bodied spindle with a blunt head, large round pectorals that scull, and a long low dorsal. */
export const KOI: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 0.75,
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Blunt head: fills out fast, then holds its depth through the shoulders.
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.11 * Math.pow(Math.sin(((s / 0.12) * Math.PI) / 2), 0.5);
      if (s < 0.42) return 0.11 + 0.01 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.42, s);
      // One continuous taper from the shoulders to a short peduncle, then a thick stalk into the fin.
      if (s < 0.76) return 0.12 - 0.07 * smoothstep(0.42, 0.76, s);
      if (s < 0.82) return 0.05 - 0.01 * smoothstep(0.76, 0.82, s);
      return 0.04 - 0.005 * smoothstep(0.82, 1, s);
    },
    // Large round paddles just behind the head: the tip sits behind the leading root, so they fan sideways.
    pectoral: [0.12, 0.26, 0.36, 0.25],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0.44, 0.52, 0.6, 0.12],
    caudal: 0,
    dorsal: [0.3, 0.62],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.06, 0.09],
    gills: [0.13, 0.17],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    tail: { kind: "caudal", length: 0.24, width: 0.07, twist: 0.06 },
    undulation: 0.55,
    finFlap: 0.25,
    finFlapRate: 1.4,
  },
  coats,
  defaultCoat: "kohaku",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/bottlenose.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const CAPE: Oklch = [0.42, 0.02, 245];
const FLANK: Oklch = [0.62, 0.015, 240];
const BEAK: Oklch = [0.52, 0.018, 245];

/** Dark grey cape over the back, lighter flanks, and a darker beak. Fins take the cape colour. */
const bottlenoseCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [CAPE, FLANK, BEAK],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
    if (p.s < 0.06) return 2;
    // The cape narrows behind the dorsal, down to across 0.35 toward the tail stock.
    const cape = 0.5 - 0.15 * smoothstep(0.52, 0.7, p.s);
    return p.across < cape ? 0 : 1;
  },
};

/** Bottlenose dolphin: a short beak, a round melon, a long barely-bending body, pointed flippers, and wide flukes. */
export const BOTTLENOSE: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 3,
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Short beak, then the melon swells up behind it.
      if (s < 0.04) return 0.025;
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.025 + 0.06 * smoothstep(0.04, 0.12, s);
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.085 + 0.015 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.3, s);
      // Tapers into a narrow tail stock.
      if (s < 0.86) return 0.1 - 0.08 * Math.pow((s - 0.3) / 0.56, 1.3);
      return 0.02 - 0.005 * smoothstep(0.86, 1, s);
    },
    pectoral: [0.2, 0.28, 0.36, 0.18],
    falcate: true,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    caudal: 0,
    dorsal: [0.42, 0.52],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.1, 0.12],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [0.95, 0.98, 1.04],
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.15, length: 0.18, notch: 0.03 },
    undulation: 0.15,
    finFlap: 0.03,
  },
  coats: { bottlenose: bottlenoseCoat },
  defaultCoat: "bottlenose",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/orca.ts
```ts
import { smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const BLACK: Oklch = [0.17, 0.01, 260];
const WHITE: Oklch = [0.96, 0.005, 250];
const SADDLE_GREY: Oklch = [0.6, 0.01, 250];

/**
 * Killer whale, black base. A white eye patch sits behind and above each eye, and a grey saddle
 * patch sits just behind the dorsal fin. Fins are black. Rolled over, the white belly shows.
 */
const orcaCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [BLACK, WHITE, SADDLE_GREY],
  paint: (p: PaintPoint) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) return 0;
    // Eye patch: an oval behind the eye on both flanks, drawn a little larger than life (s 0.11–0.25,
    // across 0.3 to the edge) so it still reads as the orca's mark at a few cells per body width.
    const ds = (p.s - 0.18) / 0.07;
    const da = (p.across - 0.68) / 0.38;
    if (ds * ds + da * da <= 1) return 1;
    // Rolling over shows the underside: the white belly grows outward from the centre line as bellyAmount rises.
    if (p.across < 0.75 * p.bellyAmount) return 1;
    // Saddle: grey where across is under a soft edge that is widest at its centre (s 0.525).
    if (p.s >= 0.45 && p.s <= 0.6 && p.across < 0.7 - (0.3 * Math.abs(p.s - 0.525)) / 0.075) {
      return 2;
    }
    return 0;
  },
};

/** Killer whale: a robust torpedo with a blunt head, big paddle flippers, a tall dorsal and a black-and-white coat. */
export const ORCA: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 7,
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Blunt head: fills out fast, then swells to the broadest point near 0.3 of the length.
      if (s < 0.02) return 0.05 + 0.01 * smoothstep(0, 0.02, s);
      if (s < 0.12) return 0.06 + 0.04 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.12, s);
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.1 + 0.025 * smoothstep(0.12, 0.3, s);
      // Long taper into a slim tail stock, then a thin peduncle to the flukes.
      if (s < 0.84) return 0.125 - 0.097 * Math.pow((s - 0.3) / 0.54, 1.2);
      return 0.028 - 0.008 * smoothstep(0.84, 1, s);
    },
    pectoral: [0.14, 0.24, 0.32, 0.2],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    caudal: 0,
    dorsal: [0.36, 0.45],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.1, 0.12],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.14, length: 0.17, notch: 0.03 },
    undulation: 0.15,
    finFlap: 0.03,
  },
  coats: { orca: orcaCoat },
  defaultCoat: "orca",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/humpback.ts
```ts
import { hash3, smoothstep } from "../math";
import { blobs } from "./paint";
import type { Coat, CreatureDef, Oklch, PaintPoint } from "./types";

// The renderer's cell flags. Importing them from the renderer would make a cycle, so they are repeated here.
const FLAG_FIN = 3;

const BACK: Oklch = [0.3, 0.012, 250];
const FIN_WHITE: Oklch = [0.92, 0.006, 240];
const KNOB: Oklch = [0.2, 0.01, 250];
const BARNACLE: Oklch = [0.8, 0.01, 250];
/** A faint lighter blotch on the back, and the dorsal hump: low contrast on purpose. */
const MOTTLE: Oklch = [0.36, 0.012, 250];

/** Fine pale specks (barnacles) where a fine noise field runs high, so they cluster rather than scatter. */
function speck(p: PaintPoint, scale: number, salt: number, cut: number) {
  return blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, scale, salt) > cut;
}

/** Head tubercles: a sparse grid of cells 0.03 L square; a knob sits near each cell's hashed centre. Only on the head. */
function tubercle(p: PaintPoint) {
  if (p.s >= 0.2 || p.across >= 0.8) return false;
  const cell = 0.03 * p.L;
  const cx = (p.s * p.L) / cell;
  const cy = p.v / cell;
  const i = Math.floor(cx);
  const j = Math.floor(cy);
  // Hashed centre, kept inside its cell so a knob never straddles two cells.
  const hx = i + 0.2 + 0.6 * hash3(i, j, p.id * 3 + 11);
  const hy = j + 0.2 + 0.6 * hash3(i, j, p.id * 3 + 12);
  const dx = cx - hx;
  const dy = cy - hy;
  return dx * dx + dy * dy < 0.25 * 0.25;
}

/**
 * Humpback, dark back with huge white flippers. The flippers are freckled, with a knobbly leading
 * edge; the head is studded with tubercles and barnacles, and two blowhole marks sit behind it. The back is
 * faintly mottled, with a lighter hump. Flukes are back-coloured (seen from above), with a pale serrated trailing edge.
 */
const humpbackCoat: Coat = {
  regions: [BACK, FIN_WHITE, KNOB, BARNACLE, MOTTLE],
  paint: (p) => {
    if (p.flag === FLAG_FIN) {
      // The flukes: a pale serrated trailing edge, alternating every 0.015 of the length, and a few barnacles on the edges.
      if (p.s > 0.84) {
        if (p.s > 0.9 && p.across > 0.6 && Math.floor(p.v / (0.015 * p.L)) % 2 === 0) return 1;
        if (p.across > 0.85 && speck(p, 0.012, 19, 0.8)) return 3;
        return 0;
      }
      // Only the flippers are white; other fins keep the back colour.
      if (p.s < 0.22 || p.s > 0.52) return 0;
      // Barnacles cluster on the flipper tips and along the leading edge.
      if ((p.s > 0.44 || p.across > 0.85) && speck(p, 0.012, 13, 0.8)) return 3;
      // Knobbly leading edge: the outermost cells alternate dark every 0.02 of the length.
      if (p.across > 0.85 && Math.floor(p.s / 0.02) % 2 === 0) return 0;
      // Freckles.
      if (blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.03, 9) > 0.82) return 0;
      return 1;
    }
    // The blowhole splashguard: a short dark pair either side of the centre line, just behind the snout.
    const vl = Math.abs(p.v) / p.L;
    if (Math.abs(p.s - 0.13) < 0.016 && vl > 0.012 && vl < 0.04) return 2;
    if (tubercle(p)) return 2;
    // Barnacles on the head.
    if (p.s < 0.2 && speck(p, 0.015, 17, 0.78)) return 3;
    // The dorsal hump is a touch lighter than the back, so its ridge reads.
    if (p.s > 0.6 && p.s < 0.68 && p.across < 0.25) return 4;
    // Faint lighter mottling across the back, on a large scale.
    if (p.s > 0.2 && p.s < 0.8 && blobs(p.s, p.v / p.L, p.id, 0.2, 21) > 0.62) return 4;
    return 0;
  },
};

/** Humpback whale: a long, robust body with a broad flat head, very long flippers, and a small dorsal hump. */
export const HUMPBACK: CreatureDef = {
  realLength: 14,
  shape: {
    halfWidth: (s) => {
      // Rostrum, then the broad flat head widens to its full depth by 0.15.
      if (s < 0.15) return 0.05 + 0.05 * smoothstep(0.01, 0.15, s);
      if (s < 0.3) return 0.1 + 0.025 * smoothstep(0.15, 0.3, s);
      if (s < 0.45) return 0.125 + 0.005 * smoothstep(0.3, 0.45, s);
      // Long taper into the tail stock, then a thin peduncle to the flukes.
      if (s < 0.84) return 0.13 - 0.1 * Math.pow((s - 0.45) / 0.39, 1.2);
      return 0.03 - 0.008 * smoothstep(0.84, 1, s);
    },
    // Very long flippers, about 30% of the length, swept back like wings.
    pectoral: [0.22, 0.29, 0.52, 0.3],
    paddle: true,
    falcate: false,
    pelvic: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    caudal: 0,
    // A small hump two-thirds of the way back.
    dorsal: [0.62, 0.66],
    secondDorsal: [0, 0],
    eye: [0.16, 0.18],
    gills: [0, 0],
    tint: [1, 1, 1],
    tail: { kind: "fluke", span: 0.17, length: 0.15, notch: 0.025 },
    undulation: 0.1,
    finFlap: 0.05,
    finFlapRate: 0.35,
  },
  coats: { humpback: humpbackCoat },
  defaultCoat: "humpback",
};
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/roster.ts
```ts
import type { Rng } from "../math";
import type { SharkSpec } from "../world";
import { CREATURES } from "./index";
import type { CreatureDef, CreatureGroup, CreatureSpecies, OceanScale } from "./types";

/** One animal to place in the water. `coat` names its colour scheme, for species that have coats. */
export type RosterEntry = { species: CreatureSpecies; rel: number; depth: number; coat?: string };

/** Most animals of one species a single group may place. */
export const MAX_PER_GROUP = 12;
/** Most animals in the whole roster. */
export const MAX_CREATURES = 16;
/** Exponent that compresses real length ratios; `stylized` softens them, `true` keeps them. */
export const SCALE_EXPONENT: Record<OceanScale, number> = { stylized: 0.45, true: 1 };

const JITTER = 0.16;

/**
 * Turns a group's variety into a coat for the species. A string is the coat (or the default when it is
 * unknown); an array is returned as the names to pick from, one per animal; no variety gives the default.
 */
function resolveCoat(
  species: CreatureSpecies,
  def: CreatureDef,
  variety: string | string[] | undefined,
  warnings: string[],
): string | string[] | undefined {
  const coats = def.coats;
  if (!coats) return undefined;
  const fallback = def.defaultCoat;
  if (typeof variety === "string") {
    if (Object.hasOwn(coats, variety)) return variety;
    warnings.push(`${species} variety "${variety}" is unknown; using ${fallback ?? "no coat"}`);
    return fallback;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(variety)) {
    const known: string[] = [];
    for (const name of variety) {
      if (Object.hasOwn(coats, name)) known.push(name);
      else warnings.push(`${species} variety "${name}" is unknown; skipped`);
    }
    if (known.length > 0) return known;
    return fallback === undefined ? undefined : [fallback];
  }
  return fallback;
}

/** Legacy `sharks` prop: length = base × size, depth = size. No randomness. */
export function rosterFromSharks(sharks: SharkSpec[]): RosterEntry[] {
  return sharks.map((s) => ({ species: s.kind, rel: s.size, depth: s.size }));
}

/**
 * `creatures` prop. rel = (realLength / largest realLength in the roster)^exponent × size × jitter,
 * jitter = 1 + (rng() − 0.5) × 0.16 (±8%). depth = rel / largest rel (so the biggest is nearest).
 * Counts are clamped to 1–MAX_PER_GROUP and the total to MAX_CREATURES; returns the warnings it raised.
 */
export function rosterFromGroups(
  groups: CreatureGroup[],
  scale: OceanScale,
  rng: Rng,
): { entries: RosterEntry[]; warnings: string[] } {
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  // Pass 1: settle counts, sizes and coats, so the largest animal is known before any rng call.
  const planned: { species: CreatureSpecies; size: number; coat?: string | string[] }[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  for (const group of groups) {
    const { species } = group;
    if (!Object.hasOwn(CREATURES, species)) {
      warnings.push(`unknown species "${species}"; skipped`);
      continue;
    }
    let count = group.count ?? 1;
    if (!Number.isFinite(count)) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} is not finite; using 1`);
      count = 1;
    } else if (!Number.isInteger(count)) {
      const floored = Math.floor(count);
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} is not an integer; using ${floored}`);
      count = floored;
    }
    if (count < 1) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count below 1; using 1`);
      count = 1;
    }
    if (count > MAX_PER_GROUP) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} exceeds ${MAX_PER_GROUP}; using ${MAX_PER_GROUP}`);
      count = MAX_PER_GROUP;
    }
    const room = MAX_CREATURES - total;
    if (count > room) {
      warnings.push(`${species} count ${count} exceeds the ${MAX_CREATURES} total; using ${room}`);
      count = room;
    }

    let size = group.size ?? 1;
    if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0) {
      warnings.push(`${species} size ${size} must be a positive number; using 1`);
      size = 1;
    }

    const coat = resolveCoat(species, CREATURES[species], group.variety, warnings);
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) planned.push({ species, size, coat });
    total += count;
  }

  if (planned.length === 0) return { entries: [], warnings };

  const exponent = SCALE_EXPONENT[scale];
  const largest = Math.max(...planned.map((p) => CREATURES[p.species].realLength));

  // Pass 2: one rng call per animal, in roster order. A coat list takes a second call after the jitter.
  const entries: RosterEntry[] = planned.map((p) => {
    const jitter = 1 + (rng() - 0.5) * JITTER;
    const rel = (CREATURES[p.species].realLength / largest) ** exponent * p.size * jitter;
    const coat = Array.isArray(p.coat) ? p.coat[Math.floor(rng() * p.coat.length)] : p.coat;
    return { species: p.species, rel, depth: rel, coat };
  });

  const nearest = Math.max(...entries.map((e) => e.rel));
  for (const e of entries) e.depth = e.rel / nearest;

  return { entries, warnings };
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/behaviour.ts
```ts
import type { CreatureSpecies } from "./types";

export type ActionId =
  | "approachAndBite"
  | "surfaceSlurp"
  | "circleThenLunge"
  | "breach"
  | "scanSweep"
  | "scanAndPin"
  | "gulpAll"
  | "burst"
  | "tailWhip"
  | "suction"
  | "slowPeck"
  | "leap"
  | "fishWhack"
  | "carousel"
  | "spyhop"
  | "bubbleNet"
  | "lunge"
  | "blowAndFluke"
  | "breachRoll"
  | "soundDive";

export interface Behaviour {
  /** How far it notices food, as a fraction of the world's shorter side. Sharks smell it from far away. */
  senseRange: number;
  /** At most this many of the species answer one offering (koi crowd in). */
  maxResponders: number;
  /** Seconds before it answers food again: [min, max]. */
  cooldown: [number, number];
  /** Weighted actions; later rounds add signature moves with `requires`. */
  actions: { id: ActionId; weight: number }[];
  /** Hunts the bait fish like the sharks do. Koi and humpbacks don't. */
  huntsFish: boolean;
  /** Drifts after the pointer when it hovers nearby (koi learn people mean food). */
  followsPointer: boolean;
  /** Breathes at the surface every [min, max] seconds (cetaceans); absent for fish and sharks. */
  breathes?: [number, number];
  /**
   * Breathes in series instead of singly (orca, humpback): a run of blows `gap` seconds apart, then a
   * dive of `dive` seconds with no breath. `fluke` raises the flukes into a sounding dive before it.
   * Each count and time comes from the creature's id and the clock, not rng.
   */
  breathSeries?: {
    blows: [number, number];
    gap: [number, number];
    dive: [number, number];
    fluke: boolean;
  };
  /**
   * Food-less shows it puts on by itself while cruising, as "every [min, max] seconds" (see world.ts stepShows).
   * Each is an action started with no food: a leap or a breach. Absent for species with no shows.
   */
  shows?: { id: ActionId; every: [number, number] }[];
  /** Keeps company with its own species in cruise: cohesion and alignment toward pod mates. */
  pod?: boolean;
  /** Most bend per spine joint (radians) in follow-the-leader; default 0.16. */
  maxBend?: number;
  /** Smallest turning radius in body lengths while cruising (not hunting or going for food); default 0.22 (the classic shark agility). */
  turnRadius?: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR: Behaviour = {
  senseRange: 0.9,
  maxResponders: 2,
  cooldown: [6, 10],
  actions: [{ id: "approachAndBite", weight: 1 }],
  huntsFish: true,
  followsPointer: false,
};

export const BEHAVIOURS: Partial<Record<CreatureSpecies, Behaviour>> = {
  koi: {
    // Koi hear pellets land anywhere in a garden pond.
    senseRange: 0.85,
    maxResponders: 5,
    cooldown: [2, 4],
    actions: [{ id: "surfaceSlurp", weight: 1 }],
    huntsFish: false,
    followsPointer: true,
    maxBend: 0.09,
    turnRadius: 0.5,
  },
  // Dolphins and whales: bottlenose travel in pods and leap or whack bait fish; orcas hunt in pods.
  bottlenose: {
    ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR,
    senseRange: 0.7,
    maxResponders: 3,
    cooldown: [4, 7],
    actions: [
      { id: "approachAndBite", weight: 0.4 },
      { id: "leap", weight: 0.35 },
      { id: "fishWhack", weight: 0.25 },
    ],
    breathes: [8, 14],
    pod: true,
    shows: [{ id: "leap", every: [18, 32] }],
  },
  orca: {
    ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR,
    senseRange: 0.75,
    maxResponders: 4,
    cooldown: [6, 10],
    actions: [
      { id: "carousel", weight: 0.6 },
      { id: "approachAndBite", weight: 0.25 },
      { id: "spyhop", weight: 0.15 },
    ],
    breathes: [12, 20],
    breathSeries: { blows: [3, 5], gap: [4, 6], dive: [15, 25], fluke: false },
    pod: true,
    shows: [{ id: "breachRoll", every: [45, 75] }],
  },
  // Humpbacks feed on krill, not fish, and answer food one at a time.
  humpback: {
    ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR,
    senseRange: 0.7,
    maxResponders: 1,
    cooldown: [10, 15],
    huntsFish: false,
    actions: [
      { id: "bubbleNet", weight: 0.6 },
      { id: "lunge", weight: 0.25 },
      { id: "blowAndFluke", weight: 0.15 },
    ],
    breathes: [18, 30],
    breathSeries: { blows: [3, 4], gap: [5, 7], dive: [25, 40], fluke: true },
    shows: [{ id: "breachRoll", every: [55, 95] }],
  },
  // Signature feeding moves, one or two per shark (docs/ascii-ocean-species.md §8.3).
  classic: {
    ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR,
    actions: [
      { id: "circleThenLunge", weight: 0.75 },
      { id: "breach", weight: 0.25 },
    ],
  },
  hammerhead: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, actions: [{ id: "scanSweep", weight: 1 }] },
  greathammer: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, actions: [{ id: "scanAndPin", weight: 1 }] },
  tiger: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, actions: [{ id: "gulpAll", weight: 1 }] },
  mako: {
    ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR,
    actions: [
      { id: "burst", weight: 0.7 },
      { id: "breach", weight: 0.3 },

  },

      { id: "tailWhip", weight: 0.8 },
      { id: "approachAndBite", weight: 0.2 },
    ],
  },
  nurse: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, actions: [{ id: "suction", weight: 1 }] },
  zebra: { ...DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR, actions: [{ id: "slowPeck", weight: 1 }] },
};

/** How a species behaves around food and the pointer. Species without an entry use DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR. */
export function behaviourOf(species: CreatureSpecies): Behaviour {
  return BEHAVIOURS[species] ?? DEFAULT_BEHAVIOUR;
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/creatures/actions.ts
```ts
import { clamp, smoothstep } from "../math";
import type { Creature, Morsel, WorldEvent } from "../world";
import type { ActionId } from "./behaviour";

/** Steering a creature gets from its action this step; w = 0 means none. */
export interface Aim {
  /** Unit direction toward the target. */
  dx: number;
  dy: number;
  /** Weight of the steering against the creature's own momentum. */
  w: number;
  /** Speed as a multiple of cruise. */
  speed: number;
  /** 0 far → 1 close to the target, for the turn and the wake. */
  closing: number;
  hunting: boolean;
}

/** Pose targets an action writes; the world eases each creature's pose toward them. */
export interface PoseTargets {
  height: number;
  pitch: number;
  roll: number;
  gape: number;
  slap: number;
  /** Sinking under the surface, 0–1: the body reads as deeper (dimmer, bluer). */
  dive: number;
  /** Head-first surface crossing, 0 under → 1 out → 2 under again (see Creature.emerge). */
  emerge: number;
}

export interface ActionContext {
  readonly time: number;
  readonly dt: number;
  readonly L: number;
  readonly sh: Creature;
  /** Seconds since the action engaged (see `engageRange`); 0 before. */
  readonly t: number;
  /** Nearest live morsel of the creature's food group, and the snout's distance to it. */
  readonly target: Morsel | null;
  readonly dist: number;
  /** World size in px, for `requires` checks such as keeping a leap on screen. */
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  /** Write this step's steering: direction (unit), weight, speed multiple of cruise, hunting flag. */
  readonly aim: Aim;
  /** Write pose targets; the world eases the creature's pose toward them. Reset to 0 every step before step() runs. */
  readonly pose: PoseTargets;
  /** Eat every live morsel of the group, and every stunned bait fish, within `radius` of the snout; returns how many. */
  eatNear(radius: number): number;
  /** Pull live morsels of the group within `radius` toward the snout at `speed` px/s (suction). */
  pullMorsels(radius: number, speed: number): void;
  /** Stun bait fish within `radius` of (x, y) for `seconds`; returns how many. */
  stunFish(x: number, y: number, radius: number, seconds: number): number;
  /** Herd live bait fish within `radius` of (x, y) this step: pull them in at `inward` px/s² and swirl them at `swirl` px/s². */
  herd(x: number, y: number, radius: number, inward: number, swirl: number): void;
  /** Eat every live bait fish within `radius` of the snout, stunned or not; returns how many. */
  eatFishNear(radius: number): number;
  /** Creatures of this species (this one included) with snouts within `radius` of the snout. */
  packNear(radius: number): number;
  /** Centroid of live bait fish within `radius` of (x, y), written into `out`; returns the count (0 = none). */
  schoolNear(
    x: number,
    y: number,
    radius: number,
    out: { x: number; y: number },
    stunnedOnly?: boolean,
  ): number;
  emit(kind: WorldEvent["kind"], x: number, y: number, size?: number): void;
}

export interface ActionDef {
  /** Snout distance to the target at which the action engages; 0 = on the first bite (as today). */
  engageRange: (L: number) => number;
  /** Seconds the action lasts once engaged. */
  seconds: number;
  /** Optional precondition checked when the action is picked; false falls back to approachAndBite. */
  requires?: (ctx: ActionContext) => boolean;
  /** Distance the engagement check uses, when it is not the snout's distance to the nearest morsel. */
  engageDistance?: (ctx: ActionContext) => number;
  /** Approach speed before engagement, as a multiple of cruise. Default 2.2, or 1.7 for koi. */
  approachSpeed?: number;
  /** Runs every step from engagement until `seconds` pass or it returns true (done). Before engagement the world runs the default approach. */
  step(ctx: ActionContext): boolean | void;
}

/** Steers at the target: a unit direction, with weight and turn closing as the snout nears it. */
function steerAt(ctx: ActionContext, speed: number) {
  const { aim, L, dist } = ctx;
  const m = ctx.target;
  if (!m) return;
  const hx = ctx.sh.nodes[0];
  const hy = ctx.sh.nodes[1];
  const closing = clamp(1 - dist / (L * 0.9), 0, 1);
  aim.dx = (m.x - hx) / dist;
  aim.dy = (m.y - hy) / dist;
  aim.w = 3 + 2 * closing;
  aim.hunting = true;
  aim.closing = closing;
  aim.speed = speed;
}

/** Bite radius shared by every action: close enough to take a morsel. */
const biteRange = (L: number) => L * 0.06 + 10;

/**
 * Whether the action engages this step: the engagement distance is within engageRange, or, for
 * engageRange 0, within a bite (the first bite engages).
 */
export function engaged(def: ActionDef, ctx: ActionContext): boolean {
  const range = def.engageRange(ctx.L);
  const reach = range > 0 ? range : biteRange(ctx.L);
  const dist = def.engageDistance ? def.engageDistance(ctx) : ctx.dist;
  return dist <= reach;
}

/** The default approach, before engagement: steer at the nearest morsel at the action's approach speed. */
export function approachSteer(def: ActionDef, ctx: ActionContext) {
  steerAt(ctx, def.approachSpeed ?? (ctx.sh.species === "koi" ? 1.7 : 2.2));
}

/** Strike radius for the lunges, bursts and sweeps: a little wider than the bite. */
const strikeRange = (L: number) => L * 0.07 + 10;

/** Scratch for schoolNear, so the whip allocates nothing. */
const school = { x: 0, y: 0 };

/** Tail sweep from 0 through the top of the arc (slap 1), then on to -0.3: sin(WHIP_SWING) = -0.3, monotonic after the peak. */
const WHIP_SWING = Math.PI + Math.asin(0.3);

/**
 * Slap pose of a whip that swings over `dur` seconds at `amp` (the swing's own overdrive). After `dur` it is held
 * at the swing's end, then eased back to rest over 0.4 s, so the fluke never drops from a slap to rest in one step.
 */
function whipSlap(u: number, dur: number, amp: number) {
  const swing = amp * Math.sin(Math.min(1, u / dur) * WHIP_SWING);
  return clamp(swing * (1 - smoothstep(dur, dur + 0.4, u)), -1.2, 1.2);
}

/** Points the snout at (px, py): unit direction, weight, speed multiple of cruise, and closing from the target. */
function aimAt(ctx: ActionContext, px: number, py: number, w: number, speed: number) {
  const { aim, L, dist, sh } = ctx;
  const dx = px - sh.nodes[0];
  const dy = py - sh.nodes[1];
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
  aim.dx = dx / d;
  aim.dy = dy / d;
  aim.w = w;
  aim.speed = speed;
  aim.hunting = true;
  aim.closing = clamp(1 - dist / (L * 0.9), 0, 1);
}

/** Head sweep: the bearing to the target swings ±0.55 rad at 1.3 Hz, like a hammerhead scanning with its head. */
function aimSweep(ctx: ActionContext, speed: number) {
  const m = ctx.target;
  if (!m) return;
  const hx = ctx.sh.nodes[0];
  const hy = ctx.sh.nodes[1];
  const a = Math.atan2(m.y - hy, m.x - hx) + 0.55 * Math.sin(ctx.t * Math.PI * 2 * 1.3);
  aimAt(ctx, hx + Math.cos(a), hy + Math.sin(a), 3, speed);
}

/** True on the one step where the action's clock first reaches `at`: lets an action do something once, with no state. */
function crossed(ctx: ActionContext, at: number) {
  return ctx.t >= at && ctx.t - ctx.dt < at;
}

/** Steers at the target, or straight ahead of the snout once the food is gone, so the aim is never NaN. */
function steerGoal(ctx: ActionContext, w: number, speed: number) {
  const m = ctx.target;
  const hx = ctx.sh.nodes[0];
  const hy = ctx.sh.nodes[1];
  if (m) aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, w, speed);
  else
    aimAt(
      ctx,
      hx + Math.cos(ctx.sh.heading) * ctx.L,
      hy + Math.sin(ctx.sh.heading) * ctx.L,
      w,
      speed,
    );
}

/** Where a school-centred action works: written into `herdAt`. */
const herdAt = { x: 0, y: 0 };

/**
 * Writes the centre a school-centred action works on into `herdAt`, and returns the bait count it came from.
 * Live bait near the food comes first, then stunned bait near the snout, then the snout itself (count 0).
 */
function locateSchool(ctx: ActionContext, radius: number): number {
  const m = ctx.target;
  if (m) {
    const n = ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, radius, herdAt);
    if (n > 0) return n;
  }
  const hx = ctx.sh.nodes[0];
  const hy = ctx.sh.nodes[1];
  const n = ctx.schoolNear(hx, hy, radius, herdAt, true);
  if (n > 0) return n;
  herdAt.x = hx;
  herdAt.y = hy;
  return 0;
}

/** Distance from the snout to the school centre an action engages on; with no school near the food, to the food. */
function schoolDistance(ctx: ActionContext, radius: number): number {
  const m = ctx.target;
  if (!m || ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, radius, herdAt) === 0) return ctx.dist;
  return Math.hypot(herdAt.x - ctx.sh.nodes[0], herdAt.y - ctx.sh.nodes[1]);
}

/** A pose's value at each of its (time, value) knots. Between two knots it blends with smoothstep, so it is flat at every knot. */
type Knot = readonly [number, number];
function track(t: number, knots: readonly Knot[]): number {
  let i = 1;
  while (i < knots.length - 1 && t >= knots[i][0]) i++;
  const [t0, v0] = knots[i - 1];
  const [t1, v1] = knots[i];
  return v0 + (v1 - v0) * smoothstep(t0, t1, t);
}

/**
 * A jump's timeline, in seconds from its start. The phases are: dip (0 to `rush`), rush up (`rush` to `up`),
 * airborne (`up` to `down`), sink (`down` to `sink`), surface (`sink` to `breath`), then the blow at `breath`.
 */
interface JumpPlan {
  rush: number;
  up: number;
  down: number;
  sink: number;
  breath: number;
  seconds: number;
  /** Speed multiple for the rush up, as a multiple of cruise. */
  rushSpeed: number;
  /** Whether the jump rolls onto its side in the air and crashes back (breachRoll). */
  roll: boolean;
  /** Pitch knots across the whole jump, eased with `track`. */
  pitch: readonly Knot[];
}

/** Bottlenose leap: a short jump, out and back in. */
const LEAP_JUMP: JumpPlan = {
  rush: 0.7,
  up: 1.3,
  down: 3,
  sink: 3.7,
  breath: 4.7,
  seconds: 4.9,
  rushSpeed: 3,
  roll: false,
  pitch: [
    [0, 0],
    [0.7, -0.4],
    [1.3, 0],
    [2.15, 0.6],
    [3, -0.6],
    [3.7, -0.3],
    [4.7, 0],
    [4.9, 0],
  ],
};

/** Orca and humpback breach: a long rolling jump, landing on its side with a crash. */
const BREACH_ROLL_JUMP: JumpPlan = {
  rush: 1.0,
  up: 1.9,
  down: 4.2,
  sink: 5.3,
  breath: 6.5,
  seconds: 6.7,
  rushSpeed: 2.6,
  roll: true,
  pitch: [
    [0, 0],
    [1.0, -0.4],
    [1.9, 0],
    [3.05, 0.6],
    [4.2, -0.6],
    [5.3, -0.3],
    [6.5, 0],
    [6.7, 0],
  ],
};

/**
 * How far the body is out of the water, 0 under → 1 out → 2 under again. Each end is a smoothstep, so the
 * head-first crossing runs over the whole rise (up − 0.35 to up + 0.5) and fall (down − 0.35 to down + 0.5).
 */
function emergeOf(p: JumpPlan, t: number) {
  return smoothstep(p.up - 0.35, p.up + 0.5, t) + smoothstep(p.down - 0.35, p.down + 0.5, t);
}

/** True on the step where the emerge value first reaches `v`, from last step's `prev` to this step's `now`. */
function reachedEmerge(prev: number, now: number, v: number) {
  return prev < v && now >= v;
}

/**
 * Runs one jump (plan `p`): dip, rush up, break out, airborne arc, re-enter, sink, surface and blow. Every pose
 * is a continuous function of the clock. Food eaten in the air is taken, so the food-driven leap still eats.
 */
function jump(ctx: ActionContext, p: JumpPlan): boolean {
  const { L, sh, t } = ctx;
  // Airborne: the height is a half sine over the arc, zero at both ends.
  const air = clamp((t - p.up) / (p.down - p.up), 0, 1);
  ctx.pose.height = Math.sin(Math.PI * air);
  // Dips under at the start and sinks after landing; it rises to the surface in between.
  ctx.pose.dive =
    smoothstep(0, 0.3, t) * (1 - smoothstep(p.rush, p.up, t)) +
    smoothstep(p.down, p.down + 0.4, t) * (1 - smoothstep(p.sink, p.breath, t));
  ctx.pose.pitch = track(t, p.pitch);
  const emerge = emergeOf(p, t);
  const prevEmerge = emergeOf(p, t - ctx.dt);
  ctx.pose.emerge = emerge;
  if (p.roll) {
    // Twists onto its side in the air, then rolls back as it sinks.
    ctx.pose.roll = 0.9 * smoothstep(0.2, 0.8, air) * (1 - smoothstep(p.down, p.sink, t));
  }
  // Dip, rush, airborne, then sink and surface: the speed steers the snout to the food, or ahead of it.
  const speed = t < p.rush ? 1 : t < p.up ? p.rushSpeed : t < p.down ? 2 : 1.6;
  steerGoal(ctx, 3, speed);
  if (t >= p.up && t < p.down) {
    // Takes what lies under the arc at any point in the air.
    ctx.eatNear(0.4 * L);
  }
  // Splashes and ripples follow the surface line: the snout breaks out and goes back in, the tail clears and goes under.
  const tail = sh.nodes.length - 2;
  if (reachedEmerge(prevEmerge, emerge, 0.05)) ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[0], sh.nodes[1]);
  if (reachedEmerge(prevEmerge, emerge, 0.95))
    ctx.emit("ripple", sh.nodes[tail], sh.nodes[tail + 1], 0.5 * L);
  if (reachedEmerge(prevEmerge, emerge, 1.05)) ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[0], sh.nodes[1]);
  if (reachedEmerge(prevEmerge, emerge, 1.95))
    ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[tail], sh.nodes[tail + 1]);
  if (p.roll && reachedEmerge(prevEmerge, emerge, 1.5)) {
    // The crash: a big splash where the mid-spine comes down.
    // The mid-spine node: half the coordinates in (the spine has an even count of x and y).
    const mid = sh.nodes.length >> 1;
    ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[mid], sh.nodes[mid + 1], 1.6 * L);
  }
  // Takes a breath after the jump, at the blowhole (spine node 2, as blowAndFluke).
  if (crossed(ctx, p.breath)) ctx.emit("blow", sh.nodes[4], sh.nodes[5], L);
  return t >= p.seconds;
}

export const ACTIONS: Record<ActionId, ActionDef> = {
  approachAndBite: {
    engageRange: () => 0,
    seconds: 3,
    step(ctx) {
      if (!ctx.target) return true;
      const bite = biteRange(ctx.L);
      if (ctx.dist <= bite) {
        ctx.eatNear(bite);
        return;
      }
      // Rushes at the food, then slows to the bite speed inside 1.2 body lengths.
      const rush = ctx.sh.species === "koi" ? 1.7 : 2.2;
      steerAt(ctx, ctx.dist > 1.2 * ctx.L ? rush : 1.2);
    },
  },
  surfaceSlurp: {
    // Engages three lengths out, so the koi slows and opens its mouth over the pellets before the first bite.
    engageRange: (L) => 3 * L,
    seconds: 9,
    step(ctx) {
      if (!ctx.target) return true;
      const { L, dist, sh } = ctx;
      // Mouth open as it nears the pellets, closing on each bite.
      // The mouth opens across the last 0.4·L of the approach, rather than in one step at 1.2·L.
      ctx.pose.gape = smoothstep(1.6 * L, 1.2 * L, dist);
      // A koi circles the pellets at about 0.22·L in radius, too wide to bite on the way past, so the slurp reaches
      // wider than a bite.
      const bite = L * 0.2 + 10;
      if (dist <= bite) {
        // Each bite breaks the surface.
        if (ctx.eatNear(bite) > 0) ctx.emit("ripple", sh.nodes[0], sh.nodes[1], L);
        return;
      }
      // Koi glide in, easing off over the last body length and slowing right down over the pellets.
      const far = sh.species === "koi" ? 1.7 : 1.6;
      const speed = 0.6 + (far - 0.6) * clamp((dist - 0.3 * L) / (0.9 * L), 0, 1);
      steerAt(ctx, speed);
    },
  },
  // Great white: circles its prey at arm's length, sizing it up, then turns in and strikes.
  circleThenLunge: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.6 * L,
    seconds: 6,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      const { L, dist, sh, t } = ctx;
      if (t < 2.6) {
        // Orbit at 0.7·L: tangent to the bearing from the prey, pulled back toward that radius.
        const rx = (sh.nodes[0] - m.x) / (dist || 1);
        const ry = (sh.nodes[1] - m.y) / (dist || 1);
        const pull = clamp((0.7 * L - dist) / L, -1, 1);
        const tx = (-ry * sh.orbitDir + rx * pull) * L;
        const ty = (rx * sh.orbitDir + ry * pull) * L;
        aimAt(ctx, sh.nodes[0] + tx, sh.nodes[1] + ty, 3, 1.5);
        return;
      }
      // Strike: straight at the prey, mouth opening as it closes in. The bite runs until the food is gone.
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 5, 2.6);
      // The mouth comes open over the first 0.4 s of the strike, rather than stepping from closed to open.
      ctx.pose.gape = clamp(1 - dist / (0.8 * L), 0, 1) * smoothstep(2.6, 3.0, t);
      ctx.eatNear(strikeRange(L));
    },
  },
  // Great white and mako: rockets up from below, clears the surface, and falls back with a splash.
  breach: {
    engageRange: (L) => 0.8 * L,
    seconds: 3,
    requires(ctx) {
      // The whole leap has to fit on screen, so the target stays at least half a length from each edge.
      const m = ctx.target;
      const edge = 0.5 * ctx.L;
      return (
        m !== null && m.x > edge && m.x < ctx.width - edge && m.y > edge && m.y < ctx.height - edge
      );
    },
    step(ctx) {
      // Once the food is eaten the target is null; the breach still lands.
      const m = ctx.target;
      const { L, sh, t } = ctx;
      // The poses blend across the phases, so none of them steps at 0.8 s or 1.9 s. The nose tips up as it runs in,
      // the body lifts clear of the water with the mouth open, and both ease back down as it falls.
      const lift = smoothstep(0.5, 1.1, t) * (1 - smoothstep(1.9, 2.4, t));
      ctx.pose.height = lift;
      ctx.pose.gape = 0.8 * lift;
      ctx.pose.pitch = (0.5 + 0.3 * smoothstep(0.7, 1.0, t)) * (1 - smoothstep(1.9, 2.6, t));
      if (t < 0.8) {
        // Run up from below.
        if (m) aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, 2.2);
        return;
      }
      if (t < 1.9) {
        // Airborne: it takes what lies under the arc.
        if (m) aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, 2.2);
        // Takes what lies under the arc at any point in the air.
        ctx.eatNear(L * 0.5);
        return;
      }
      // Falls back: one splash where the snout lands.
      if (crossed(ctx, 1.9)) ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[0], sh.nodes[1]);
      return t >= 2.6;
    },
  },
  // Scalloped hammerhead: sweeps its wide head across the food like a metal detector, then bites.
  scanSweep: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.2 * L,
    seconds: 7,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      if (ctx.t < 1.8) {
        aimSweep(ctx, 1);
        return;
      }
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, 1.6);
      if (ctx.dist <= strikeRange(ctx.L)) ctx.eatNear(strikeRange(ctx.L));
    },
  },
  // Great hammerhead: sweeps, then drives its head down onto the food and pins it before taking it.
  scanAndPin: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.2 * L,
    seconds: 7,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      const { L, dist, t } = ctx;
      if (t < 1.2) {
        aimSweep(ctx, 1);
        return;
      }
      if (t < 4.6) {
        // Close in; once the head is on the food it slows, pitches down and draws in the loose pieces.
        const pin = dist <= 0.15 * L;
        aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, pin ? 0.15 : 2.2);
        // The head pitches down over the last 0.15·L of the approach, not in one step at the pin.
        ctx.pose.pitch = -0.35 * smoothstep(0.3 * L, 0.15 * L, dist);
        if (pin) ctx.pullMorsels(0.3 * L, 40);
        return;
      }
      ctx.eatNear(0.35 * L);
      return true;
    },
  },
  // Tiger shark: unhurried and indiscriminate, it takes every scrap in the water, one after another.
  gulpAll: {
    engageRange: () => 0,
    seconds: 6,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      const { L, dist } = ctx;
      ctx.pose.gape = 0.6 * smoothstep(0.7 * L, 0.5 * L, dist);
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, 1.1);
      ctx.eatNear(biteRange(L));
    },
  },
  // Mako: the fastest shark in the sea. Bursts at full tilt and turns sharply to snatch its prey.
  burst: {
    engageRange: (L) => 2 * L,
    seconds: 6,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      // Full burst until the food is 0.6·L off, then eases down to 1.6× so its turn can bring the snout onto the food.
      const near = clamp((ctx.dist - 0.3 * ctx.L) / (0.3 * ctx.L), 0, 1);
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 6, 1.6 + 1.6 * near);
      ctx.aim.closing = 1;
      ctx.eatNear(strikeRange(ctx.L));
    },
  },
  // Thresher: swims past a bait ball and whips its long upper tail lobe through it, stunning fish in the arc
  // (Oliver et al. 2013). Then it doubles back and eats what is left.
  tailWhip: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.4 * L,
    seconds: 12,
    requires(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      return m !== null && ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 1.5 * ctx.L, school) >= 4;
    },
    // Engages on the distance to the school's centre; with no school in reach, to the food.
    engageDistance(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m || ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 1.5 * ctx.L, school) === 0) return ctx.dist;
      return Math.hypot(school.x - ctx.sh.nodes[0], school.y - ctx.sh.nodes[1]);
    },
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      const { L, sh, time } = ctx;
      const hx = sh.nodes[0];
      const hy = sh.nodes[1];
      // Before the whip there is nothing to aim at; after it, the food can be gone and the snout is the aim.
      if (!m && sh.actionMark < 0) return true;
      const mx = m ? m.x : hx;
      const my = m ? m.y : hy;
      const n = m ? ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 0.6 * L, school) : 0;
      const cx = n > 0 ? school.x : mx;
      const cy = n > 0 ? school.y : my;
      // The tail tip is the last spine node.
      const tail = sh.nodes.length - 2;
      // Aim point 0.3·L to the side of the school's centre and just beyond it, so the snout swims past.
      const cd = Math.hypot(cx - hx, cy - hy) || 1;
      const ux = (cx - hx) / cd;
      const uy = (cy - hy) / cd;
      const side = 0.3 * L * sh.orbitDir;
      const px = cx + ux * 0.3 * L - uy * side;
      const py = cy + uy * 0.3 * L + ux * side;
      if (sh.actionMark < 0) {
        // Still swimming in: whip once the snout has passed the school's centre.
        aimAt(ctx, px, py, 3, 1.4);
        const past = Math.cos(sh.heading) * (cx - hx) + Math.sin(sh.heading) * (cy - hy) < 0;
        if (!past) return;
        sh.actionMark = time;
      }
      const u = time - sh.actionMark;
      // Overdriven ×1.2 (target capped at ±1.2): the eased pose lags the 0.5 s swing, so the tail reaches ~0.88 of its peak.
      ctx.pose.slap = whipSlap(u, 0.5, 1.2);
      if (u < 0.5) {
        // The pectorals snap in and the shark stalls its approach as the tail comes over (Oliver et al. 2013).
        aimAt(ctx, px, py, 3, 0.5);
        // At the top of the swing the tail tip stuns the fish in its arc.
        const peak = (0.5 * (Math.PI / 2)) / WHIP_SWING;
        if (u >= peak && u - ctx.dt < peak) {
          const tx = sh.nodes[tail];
          const ty = sh.nodes[tail + 1];
          // Oliver et al. 2013: the slap stuns or kills; dazed sardines stay put long enough for the turn back.
          ctx.stunFish(tx, ty, 0.45 * L, 8);
          ctx.emit("splash", tx, ty);
        }
        return;
      }
      // Circles back for the dazed fish it left behind if there are any, otherwise for the food.
      if (ctx.schoolNear(hx, hy, 2.5 * L, school, true) > 0) {
        aimAt(ctx, school.x, school.y, 4, 1.8);
      } else {
        aimAt(ctx, mx, my, 3, 1.2);
      }
      ctx.eatNear(biteRange(L));
    },
  },
  // Nurse shark: nearly motionless, it opens its mouth and hoovers the food in with suction.
  suction: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.0 * L,
    seconds: 8,
    approachSpeed: 1,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      const { L, sh, t } = ctx;
      // Brakes hard while still moving faster than 0.35 cruise (cruise as world.ts sets it), then holds at 0.2.
      const cruise = 30 + L * 0.04;
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 2, sh.speed > 0.35 * cruise ? 0.1 : 0.2);
      ctx.pullMorsels(0.9 * L, 60);
      // The gape pulses in from closed, so it does not open with a step at the start.
      ctx.pose.gape = (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 8)) * smoothstep(0, 0.5, t);
      ctx.eatNear(L * 0.08 + 8);
    },
  },
  // Zebra shark: a slow bottom feeder that noses along and takes one morsel at a time, pausing after each.
  slowPeck: {
    engageRange: () => 0,
    seconds: 6,
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      if (!m) return true;
      const { L, dist, sh, time } = ctx;
      // actionMark is the time the next bite is allowed (-1 before the first bite).
      const resting = sh.actionMark >= 0 && time < sh.actionMark;
      aimAt(ctx, m.x, m.y, 3, resting ? 0.3 : 0.8);
      if (!resting && dist <= biteRange(L) && ctx.eatNear(biteRange(L)) > 0) {
        sh.actionMark = time + 0.6;
      }
    },
  },
  // Bottlenose dolphin: leaps clear of the water, around food or on its own (a show). See jump().
  leap: {
    engageRange: (L) => 0.8 * L,
    seconds: LEAP_JUMP.seconds,
    requires(ctx) {
      // The leap has to stay on screen, so the target keeps at least 0.8·L from each edge (the leap runs further than it did).
      const m = ctx.target;
      const edge = 0.8 * ctx.L;
      return (
        m !== null && m.x > edge && m.x < ctx.width - edge && m.y > edge && m.y < ctx.height - edge
      );
    },
    step(ctx) {
      const done = jump(ctx, LEAP_JUMP);
      // A leap with no food can porpoise: an even-parity one chains into another leap a second after it lands.
      if (done && ctx.sh.foodGroup < 0 && (ctx.sh.id + Math.floor(ctx.sh.actionStart)) % 2 === 0) {
        ctx.sh.chain = true;
        ctx.sh.nextShow = ctx.time + 1;
      }
      return done;
    },
  },
  // Orca and humpback: breaches with a roll, landing on its side or back with a huge splash. Only ever a show.
  breachRoll: {
    engageRange: () => 0,
    seconds: BREACH_ROLL_JUMP.seconds,
    step: (ctx) => jump(ctx, BREACH_ROLL_JUMP),
  },
  // Humpback sounding dive: raises its flukes clear of the water and slips under them. Only ever a show.
  soundDive: {
    engageRange: () => 0,
    seconds: 5,
    step(ctx) {
      const { sh, t } = ctx;
      // A small lift as the flukes come up, eased back to rest by the time they rise (no step at t = 1).
      ctx.pose.height = 0.2 * (1 - smoothstep(0.8, 1.2, t));
      // Pitches the head down while the flukes are up, then levels off as the fluke swings clear.
      ctx.pose.pitch = -0.8 * smoothstep(0.7, 1.3, t) * (1 - smoothstep(3.6, 4.4, t));
      ctx.pose.dive = smoothstep(1, 3.5, t);
      // The flukes rise and slip under: a slap that lifts and falls back to 0 at 1 s and at 4 s.
      ctx.pose.slap = 0.6 * Math.sin(Math.PI * clamp((t - 1) / 3, 0, 1));
      steerGoal(ctx, 3, 0.6);
      // The fluke print: a bubble at the tail as the flukes go under.
      if (crossed(ctx, 3.6)) {
        const tail = sh.nodes.length - 2;
        ctx.emit("bubble", sh.nodes[tail], sh.nodes[tail + 1]);
      }
      return t >= 4.5;
    },
  },
  // Bottlenose dolphin: swims into a bait ball and slaps its fluke through it, stunning the fish, then mops them up.
  fishWhack: {
    engageRange: (L) => 1.2 * L,
    seconds: 5,
    requires(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      return m !== null && ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 1.5 * ctx.L, school) >= 3;
    },
    step(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      const { L, sh, time } = ctx;
      const hx = sh.nodes[0];
      const hy = sh.nodes[1];
      // Before the whip there is nothing to aim at; after it, the food can be gone and the snout is the aim.
      if (!m && sh.actionMark < 0) return true;
      const mx = m ? m.x : hx;
      const my = m ? m.y : hy;
      const n = m ? ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 1.5 * L, school) : 0;
      const cx = n > 0 ? school.x : mx;
      const cy = n > 0 ? school.y : my;
      if (sh.actionMark < 0) {
        // Approach the school's centre, and whip once the snout is within 0.5·L of it.
        aimAt(ctx, cx, cy, 3, 1.6);
        if (Math.hypot(cx - hx, cy - hy) > 0.5 * L) return;
        sh.actionMark = time;
      }
      const u = time - sh.actionMark;
      ctx.pose.slap = whipSlap(u, 0.4, 1.5);
      if (u < 0.4) {
        // The body stalls as the fluke comes over, then slaps sideways through the school (same swing as the thresher).
        aimAt(ctx, cx, cy, 3, 0.5);
        // At the top of the swing the fluke tip stuns the fish in its arc, and a splash marks where they are flung.
        const peak = (0.4 * (Math.PI / 2)) / WHIP_SWING;
        if (u >= peak && u - ctx.dt < peak) {
          const tail = sh.nodes.length - 2;
          const tx = sh.nodes[tail];
          const ty = sh.nodes[tail + 1];
          // The tail's outward direction, so the splash lands 0.6·L past the tip.
          const ex = tx - sh.nodes[tail - 2];
          const ey = ty - sh.nodes[tail - 1];
          const ed = Math.hypot(ex, ey) || 1;
          ctx.stunFish(tx, ty, 0.35 * L, 6);
          ctx.emit("splash", tx + (ex / ed) * 0.6 * L, ty + (ey / ed) * 0.6 * L);
        }
        return;
      }
      // Turns back for the stunned fish if there are any, otherwise for the food, and takes what it passes.
      if (ctx.schoolNear(hx, hy, 2.5 * L, school, true) > 0) {
        aimAt(ctx, school.x, school.y, 3, 1.6);
      } else {
        aimAt(ctx, mx, my, 3, 1.6);
      }
      ctx.eatNear(biteRange(L));
    },
  },
  // Orca carousel: the pod circles a bait ball, flashing white bellies and blowing bubbles to tighten it. Then each
  // orca in turn slaps the ball with its fluke, stunning fish, and eats the stunned ones one at a time.
  carousel: {
    engageRange: (L) => 2 * L,
    seconds: 10,
    requires(ctx) {
      const m = ctx.target;
      return (
        m !== null &&
        ctx.packNear(3 * ctx.L) >= 3 &&
        ctx.schoolNear(m.x, m.y, 2 * ctx.L, herdAt) >= 6
      );
    },
    engageDistance: (ctx) => schoolDistance(ctx, 2 * ctx.L),
    step(ctx) {
      const { L, sh, t, time, dt } = ctx;
      const hx = sh.nodes[0];
      const hy = sh.nodes[1];
      const n = locateSchool(ctx, 2 * L);
      // Each orca strikes at its own turn, so it needs bait to strike at. With none left before then, the carousel ends.
      const strikeAt = 3.5 + (sh.id % 4) * 1.2;
      if (n === 0 && t < strikeAt) return true;
      const cx = herdAt.x;
      const cy = herdAt.y;
      ctx.herd(cx, cy, 1.6 * L, 60, 40);
      // A bubble at the snout every 0.35 s, staggered by id: the bubbles tighten the ball.
      const beat = (time + sh.id * 0.05) / 0.35;
      if (Math.floor(beat) !== Math.floor(beat - dt / 0.35)) ctx.emit("bubble", hx, hy);
      if (sh.actionMark < 0 && t >= strikeAt) {
        // Cuts in toward the centre; the whip starts once the snout is within 0.6·L of it, or after 1.5 s of
        // cutting in (a big orca turns wide and may never get closer).
        aimAt(ctx, cx, cy, 3, 1.6);
        if (Math.hypot(cx - hx, cy - hy) > 0.6 * L && t < strikeAt + 1.5) return;
        sh.actionMark = time;
      }
      if (sh.actionMark >= 0) {
        const u = time - sh.actionMark;
        ctx.pose.slap = whipSlap(u, 0.5, 1.5);
        if (u < 0.5) {
          // The pectorals snap in and the orca stalls as the fluke comes over (the same swing as the thresher's).
          aimAt(ctx, cx, cy, 3, 0.5);
          // At the top of the swing the fluke comes down on the ball: the fish in it are stunned, and the splash
          // rises at the fluke tip. The stun is centred on the ball, not the tip, because the turn that brings the
          // fluke over the ball is too wide to model on this spine.
          const peak = (0.5 * (Math.PI / 2)) / WHIP_SWING;
          if (u >= peak && u - dt < peak) {
            const tail = sh.nodes.length - 2;
            ctx.stunFish(cx, cy, 0.5 * L, 6);
            ctx.emit("splash", sh.nodes[tail], sh.nodes[tail + 1]);
          }
          return;
        }
        if (u < 3) {
          // Turns toward the stunned fish (the centre stays put when none are stunned) and takes them one at a time.
          ctx.schoolNear(cx, cy, 2 * L, herdAt, true);
          aimAt(ctx, herdAt.x, herdAt.y, 3, 1.2);
          ctx.eatFishNear(L * 0.15 + 10);
          return;
        }
      }
      // Circling: the whole pod goes the same way round the ball at 0.9·L, the belly rolling to flash.
      const dir = sh.foodGroup % 2 ? 1 : -1;
      const a = Math.atan2(hy - cy, hx - cx) + dir * 0.5;
      aimAt(ctx, cx + Math.cos(a) * 0.9 * L, cy + Math.sin(a) * 0.9 * L, 3, 1.2);
      // A slow roll onto one side about every 7 s, then back: a gentle belly flash, never a flip. The flash is a
```
