```ts
const FLOWER_WHITE_CHANCE = 0.8;
/** The lily's 3 by 3 cells: row -1, row 0 (the stamen `@` in the centre), and row +1. */
const FLOWER_ROWS = ["\\|/", "*@*", "/'\\"];
/** Duckweed: 4 to 7 patches of 6 to 16 fronds, each patch 0.06 to 0.14 koi lengths across. */
const PATCH_MIN = 4;
const PATCH_MAX = 7;
const FROND_MIN = 6;
const FROND_MAX = 16;
const PATCH_R_MIN = 0.06;
const PATCH_R_SPAN = 0.08;
/** Loose fronds: 10 to 20 single fronds that drift alone. */
const LOOSE_MIN = 10;
const LOOSE_MAX = 20;
/** Duckweed follows the wind at this multiple of the pad speed. */
const DUCK_SPEED = 1.3;
/** The slow wind drift, px/s: vx = WIND_X sin(RATE t + phase), vy = WIND_Y cos(RATE t + phase). */
const WIND_X = 2.2;
const WIND_X_RATE = 0.05;
const WIND_Y = 1.6;
const WIND_Y_RATE = 0.037;
/** A ripple or eat reaches PUSH_REACH_R radii plus PUSH_REACH_SIZE body lengths, and gives PUSH_SPEED px/s. */
const PUSH_SPEED = 14;
const PUSH_DECAY = 1.5;
const PUSH_REACH_R = 1.4;
const PUSH_REACH_SIZE = 0.4;
/** Pads drift back from the margins: px/s² per px past the margin. */
const EDGE_SPRING = 6;
/** A koi pushes plants aside: they take this share of its velocity, scaled by how deep the overlap is. */
const WAKE_GAIN = 0.6;
/** Pads and patches are never pushed faster than this, px/s. The decay then brings them back to the wind. */
const PAD_MAX_SPEED = 40;
/** Sub-cell sample points (x, y pairs) for pad coverage, as in the renderer. */
const SUB = [0.25, 0.3, 0.75, 0.3, 0.25, 0.72, 0.75, 0.72];
/** Shadow offset from the pad centre, in radii. The shadow falls on the floor. */
const SHADOW_DX = 0.18;
const SHADOW_DY = 0.28;
/** The shadow is black: the darker of black and half the page background is always black. */
const SHADOW_ALPHA = { dark: 0.14, light: 0.08 };
// Plant colours in OKLCH, converted once in setPalette.
const BODY_INNER: OKLCH = [0.41, 0.09, 146];
const BODY_MID: OKLCH = [0.46, 0.1, 142];
const BODY_OUTER: OKLCH = [0.5, 0.105, 140];
const RIM: OKLCH = [0.54, 0.1, 138];
const RIM_INK: OKLCH = [0.38, 0.08, 142];
const VEIN_INK: OKLCH = [0.32, 0.08, 148];
const PETAL_WHITE: OKLCH = [0.96, 0.015, 95];
// White petals would vanish on a white page; there they read as cream with a faint pink blush.
const PETAL_WHITE_LIGHT: OKLCH = [0.78, 0.05, 60];
const PETAL_PINK: OKLCH = [0.82, 0.09, 350];
const STAMEN: OKLCH = [0.85, 0.16, 95];
const DUCK: OKLCH = [0.74, 0.17, 128];
const DUCK_DARK: OKLCH = [0.66, 0.15, 132];

function css(c: OKLCH) {
  const [r, g, b] = oklchToRgb(c[0], c[1], c[2]);
  return `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
}

/** Vein glyph for a cell's direction from the pad centre: a dash, a bar, or a slash. */
function veinGlyph(dx: number, dy: number) {
  const ax = Math.abs(dx);
  const ay = Math.abs(dy);
  if (ax > 2.4 * ay) return "-";
  if (ay > 2.4 * ax) return "|";
  return dx * dy > 0 ? "\\" : "/";
}

/** Caps a body's speed at PAD_MAX_SPEED. */
function capSpeed(ax: Float64Array, ay: Float64Array, i: number) {
  const s = Math.hypot(ax[i], ay[i]);
  if (s > PAD_MAX_SPEED) {
    ax[i] *= PAD_MAX_SPEED / s;
    ay[i] *= PAD_MAX_SPEED / s;
  }
}

/** Moves one body in a ripple or eat: a radial kick of PUSH_SPEED if it lies within reach of (x, y). */
function kick(
  px: number,
  py: number,
  vx: Float64Array,
  vy: Float64Array,
  i: number,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  reach: number,
) {
  const dx = px - x;
  const dy = py - y;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (d > reach) return;
  // A push at the exact centre goes along +x.
  const ux = d > 0 ? dx / d : 1;
  const uy = d > 0 ? dy / d : 0;
  vx[i] += ux * PUSH_SPEED;
  vy[i] += uy * PUSH_SPEED;
  capSpeed(vx, vy, i);
}

/**
 * A koi's wake on one body: within radius + r, the body takes WAKE_GAIN of the koi's velocity, plus the
 * same amount outward from the koi, scaled by the overlap. Its speed is then capped at PAD_MAX_SPEED.
 */
function nudge(
  px: number,
  py: number,
  r: number,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  vx: number,
  vy: number,
  radius: number,
  ax: Float64Array,
  ay: Float64Array,
  i: number,
) {
  const reach = radius + r;
  const dx = px - x;
  const dy = py - y;
  const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
  if (d >= reach) return;
  const gain = (WAKE_GAIN * (reach - d)) / reach;
  const speed = Math.hypot(vx, vy);
  const ux = d > 0 ? dx / d : 1;
  const uy = d > 0 ? dy / d : 0;
  ax[i] += (vx + ux * speed) * gain;
  ay[i] += (vy + uy * speed) * gain;
  capSpeed(ax, ay, i);
}

/** True when cell (col, row)'s centre lies outside the disc of radius rad centred at (cx, cy). */
function outsideDisc(
  col: number,
  row: number,
  cx: number,
  cy: number,
  rad: number,
  cw: number,
  ch: number,
) {
  const dx = (col + 0.5) * cw - cx;
  const dy = (row + 0.5) * ch - cy;
  return dx * dx + dy * dy > rad * rad;
}

/** Wraps a coordinate that has drifted past one edge back in from the other, by a body of radius R. */
function wrap(p: number, span: number, R: number) {
  if (p < -R) return p + span + 2 * R;
  if (p > span + R) return p - span - 2 * R;
  return p;
}

/**
 * The pond's floating plants, drawn over the water. Pads are opaque: each has a rim, a three-shade body,
 * veins on spokes, a centre `o`, and sometimes a water lily. Koi that cruise shallow push pads aside; deeper
 * koi pass underneath and are hidden. Duckweed drifts in patches and loose fronds on the same wind.
 *
 * Call layout() on resize, then per frame: pushFrom() per koi, step(), and drawShadows() and drawPlants() in
 * the render. ripples and eats call push().
 */
export class PondSurface {
  /** Pads in use. The arrays hold the most a canvas can take; the first padCount are live. */
  padCount = 0;
  padX = new Float64Array(0);
  padY = new Float64Array(0);
  padR = new Float64Array(0);
  /** Rotation and spin of each pad, rad and rad/s. */
  padRot = new Float64Array(0);
  padSpin = new Float64Array(0);
  /** Notch centreline, the lily's direction (opposite the notch), and the spokes' start angle and count. */
  padNotch = new Float64Array(0);
  padFlowerAng = new Float64Array(0);
  padSpokeBase = new Float64Array(0);
  padSpokeN = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** 1 when the pad carries a water lily; 1 when that lily is white (else pink). */
  padFlower = new Uint8Array(0);
  padFlowerWhite = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Push velocity per pad, px/s. It decays, and the wind is added on top. */
  padVx = new Float64Array(0);
  padVy = new Float64Array(0);
  /** Duckweed patches in use: centres, radii and push velocities. */
  patchCount = 0;
  patchX = new Float64Array(0);
  patchY = new Float64Array(0);
  patchR = new Float64Array(0);
  patchVx = new Float64Array(0);
  patchVy = new Float64Array(0);
  /** Fronds in patches: the patch each belongs to, its offset from the centre, its glyph (0 `°`, 1 `·`) and its green (0 light, 1 dark). */
  frondCount = 0;
  frondPatch = new Uint8Array(0);
  frondDx = new Float32Array(0);
  frondDy = new Float32Array(0);
  frondGlyph = new Uint8Array(0);
  frondShade = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Loose fronds that drift alone: position, push velocity, glyph and green. */
  looseCount = 0;
  looseX = new Float64Array(0);
  looseY = new Float64Array(0);
  looseVx = new Float64Array(0);
  looseVy = new Float64Array(0);
  looseGlyph = new Uint8Array(0);
  looseShade = new Uint8Array(0);

  private width = 0;
  private height = 0;
  private seed = 0;
  private cursor = 0;
  private windPhaseX = 0;
  private windPhaseY = 0;
  private shadowStyle = "";
  private bodyInnerStyle = "";
  private bodyMidStyle = "";
  private bodyOuterStyle = "";
  private rimStyle = "";
  private rimInkStyle = "";
  private veinStyle = "";
  private whiteStyle = "";
  private pinkStyle = "";
  private stamenStyle = "";
  private duckStyle = "";
  private duckDarkStyle = "";

  /** Converts the plant colours once. The shadow is black at a light alpha, so it reads on both pages. */
  setPalette(mode: "light" | "dark") {
    this.shadowStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${SHADOW_ALPHA[mode]})`;
    this.bodyInnerStyle = css(BODY_INNER);
    this.bodyMidStyle = css(BODY_MID);
    this.bodyOuterStyle = css(BODY_OUTER);
    this.rimStyle = css(RIM);
    this.rimInkStyle = css(RIM_INK);
    this.veinStyle = css(VEIN_INK);
    this.whiteStyle = css(mode === "light" ? PETAL_WHITE_LIGHT : PETAL_WHITE);
    this.pinkStyle = css(PETAL_PINK);
    this.stamenStyle = css(STAMEN);
    this.duckStyle = css(DUCK);
    this.duckDarkStyle = css(DUCK_DARK);
  }

  /** Places the pads, water lilies and duckweed for a canvas of this size. bodyL is the largest koi's length in px. */
  layout(width: number, height: number, bodyL: number) {
    this.width = width;
    this.height = height;
    // An integer seed from the size, so a new size reseeds the draws.
    this.seed = (Math.round(width) * 73856093) ^ (Math.round(height) * 19349663) ^ SALT;
    this.cursor = 0;
    this.windPhaseX = this.rnd() * TAU;
    this.windPhaseY = this.rnd() * TAU;

    const want = clamp(Math.round((width * height) / PAD_AREA), PAD_MIN, PAD_MAX);
    this.padX = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padY = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padR = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padRot = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padSpin = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padNotch = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padFlowerAng = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padSpokeBase = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padSpokeN = new Uint8Array(want);
    this.padFlower = new Uint8Array(want);
    this.padFlowerWhite = new Uint8Array(want);
    this.padVx = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padVy = new Float64Array(want);
    this.padCount = 0;
    const coverLimit = PAD_COVER * width * height;
    let covered = 0;
    // Rejection: each pad gets PAD_TRIES tries to find a clear spot, then is left out. Stop once the cover is full.
    for (let i = 0; i < want && covered < coverLimit; i++) {
      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < PAD_TRIES; attempt++) {
        const r = (PAD_R_MIN + PAD_R_SPAN * this.rnd()) * bodyL;
        const u = this.rnd();
        const v = this.rnd();
        const lo = PAD_MARGIN * r;
        const spanX = width - 2 * lo;
        const spanY = height - 2 * lo;
        if (spanX < 0 || spanY < 0) continue;
        const x = lo + spanX * u;
        const y = lo + spanY * v;
        if (covered + Math.PI * r * r > coverLimit) break;
        if (this.overlapsPad(x, y, r)) continue;
        this.placePad(this.padCount++, x, y, r);
        covered += Math.PI * r * r;
        break;
      }
    }

    const patches = PATCH_MIN + Math.floor(this.rnd() * (PATCH_MAX - PATCH_MIN + 1));
    this.patchCount = patches;
    this.patchX = new Float64Array(PATCH_MAX);
    this.patchY = new Float64Array(PATCH_MAX);
    this.patchR = new Float64Array(PATCH_MAX);
    this.patchVx = new Float64Array(PATCH_MAX);
    this.patchVy = new Float64Array(PATCH_MAX);
    this.frondPatch = new Uint8Array(PATCH_MAX * FROND_MAX);
    this.frondDx = new Float32Array(PATCH_MAX * FROND_MAX);
    this.frondDy = new Float32Array(PATCH_MAX * FROND_MAX);
    this.frondGlyph = new Uint8Array(PATCH_MAX * FROND_MAX);
    this.frondShade = new Uint8Array(PATCH_MAX * FROND_MAX);
    this.frondCount = 0;
    for (let p = 0; p < patches; p++) {
      const R = (PATCH_R_MIN + PATCH_R_SPAN * this.rnd()) * bodyL;
      this.patchX[p] = this.rnd() * width;
      this.patchY[p] = this.rnd() * height;
      this.patchR[p] = R;
      const n = FROND_MIN + Math.floor(this.rnd() * (FROND_MAX - FROND_MIN + 1));
      for (let f = 0; f < n; f++) {
        const a = this.rnd() * TAU;
        // hash^1.5 pulls fronds toward the centre, so the patch is denser there.
        const d = Math.pow(this.rnd(), 1.5) * R;
        const k = this.frondCount++;
        this.frondPatch[k] = p;
        this.frondDx[k] = Math.cos(a) * d;
        this.frondDy[k] = Math.sin(a) * d;
        this.frondGlyph[k] = this.rnd() < 0.4 ? 1 : 0;
        this.frondShade[k] = this.rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1;
      }
    }

    const loose = LOOSE_MIN + Math.floor(this.rnd() * (LOOSE_MAX - LOOSE_MIN + 1));
    this.looseCount = loose;
    this.looseX = new Float64Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    this.looseY = new Float64Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    this.looseVx = new Float64Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    this.looseVy = new Float64Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    this.looseGlyph = new Uint8Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    this.looseShade = new Uint8Array(LOOSE_MAX);
    for (let k = 0; k < loose; k++) {
      this.looseX[k] = this.rnd() * width;
      this.looseY[k] = this.rnd() * height;
      this.looseGlyph[k] = this.rnd() < 0.4 ? 1 : 0;
      this.looseShade[k] = this.rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1;
    }
  }

  /** Advances the plants: the wind, pad spin, decaying pushes, pads pushed apart, and soft edges. */
  step(dt: number, t: number) {
    const { padX, padY, padR, padVx, padVy, width, height } = this;
    const n = this.padCount;
    const wx = WIND_X * Math.sin(WIND_X_RATE * t + this.windPhaseX);
    const wy = WIND_Y * Math.cos(WIND_Y_RATE * t + this.windPhaseY);
    const decay = Math.exp(-PUSH_DECAY * dt);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      this.padRot[i] += this.padSpin[i] * dt;
      padVx[i] *= decay;
      padVy[i] *= decay;
      padX[i] += (wx + padVx[i]) * dt;
      padY[i] += (wy + padVy[i]) * dt;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
        const dx = padX[j] - padX[i];
        const dy = padY[j] - padY[i];
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        const min = padR[i] + padR[j];
        if (d >= min) continue;
        // Coincident centres split along x.
        const ux = d > 0 ? dx / d : 1;
        const uy = d > 0 ? dy / d : 0;
        const half = (min - d) / 2;
        padX[i] -= ux * half;
        padY[i] -= uy * half;
        padX[j] += ux * half;
        padY[j] += uy * half;
      }
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const lo = PAD_MARGIN * padR[i];
      const hiX = width - lo;
      const hiY = height - lo;
      if (padX[i] < lo) padVx[i] += (lo - padX[i]) * EDGE_SPRING * dt;
      else if (padX[i] > hiX) padVx[i] -= (padX[i] - hiX) * EDGE_SPRING * dt;
      if (padY[i] < lo) padVy[i] += (lo - padY[i]) * EDGE_SPRING * dt;
      else if (padY[i] > hiY) padVy[i] -= (padY[i] - hiY) * EDGE_SPRING * dt;
      padX[i] = clamp(padX[i], lo, hiX);
      padY[i] = clamp(padY[i], lo, hiY);
    }

    // Duckweed rides the same wind, faster, and wraps around the edges.
    const { patchX, patchY, patchR, patchVx, patchVy } = this;
    for (let p = 0; p < this.patchCount; p++) {
      patchVx[p] *= decay;
      patchVy[p] *= decay;
      patchX[p] = wrap(patchX[p] + (DUCK_SPEED * wx + patchVx[p]) * dt, width, patchR[p]);
      patchY[p] = wrap(patchY[p] + (DUCK_SPEED * wy + patchVy[p]) * dt, height, patchR[p]);
    }
    const { looseX, looseY, looseVx, looseVy } = this;
    for (let k = 0; k < this.looseCount; k++) {
      looseVx[k] *= decay;
      looseVy[k] *= decay;
      looseX[k] = wrap(looseX[k] + (DUCK_SPEED * wx + looseVx[k]) * dt, width, 0);
      looseY[k] = wrap(looseY[k] + (DUCK_SPEED * wy + looseVy[k]) * dt, height, 0);
    }
  }

  /** A ripple set or an eat at (x, y): pads and patches within reach get a radial kick, capped in speed. */
  push(x: number, y: number, size: number) {
    const extra = PUSH_REACH_SIZE * size;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.padCount; i++) {
      kick(
        this.padX[i],
        this.padY[i],
        this.padVx,
        this.padVy,
        i,
        x,
        y,
        PUSH_REACH_R * this.padR[i] + extra,
      );
    }
    for (let p = 0; p < this.patchCount; p++) {
      kick(
        this.patchX[p],
        this.patchY[p],
        this.patchVx,
        this.patchVy,
        p,
        x,
        y,
        this.patchR[p] + extra,
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * A koi's wake at (x, y), moving at (vx, vy) px/s, with the given radius: pads, patches and loose fronds within
   * reach are nudged along the koi's motion and outward from it. Called per koi, per frame.
   */
  pushFrom(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, radius: number) {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.padCount; i++) {
      nudge(
        this.padX[i],
        this.padY[i],
        this.padR[i],
        x,
        y,
        vx,
        vy,
        radius,
        this.padVx,
        this.padVy,
        i,
      );
    }
    for (let p = 0; p < this.patchCount; p++) {
      nudge(
        this.patchX[p],
        this.patchY[p],
        this.patchR[p],
        x,
        y,
        vx,
        vy,
        radius,
        this.patchVx,
        this.patchVy,
        p,
      );
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < this.looseCount; k++) {
      nudge(this.looseX[k], this.looseY[k], 0, x, y, vx, vy, radius, this.looseVx, this.looseVy, k);
    }
  }

  /** Shadows on the floor, under the creatures: each pad's disc, offset down and to the right. */
  drawShadows(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cw: number, ch: number, cols: number, rows: number) {
    g.fillStyle = this.shadowStyle;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.padCount; i++) {
      const rad = this.padR[i];
      const sx = this.padX[i] + SHADOW_DX * rad;
      const sy = this.padY[i] + SHADOW_DY * rad;
      const c0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((sx - rad) / cw));
      const c1 = Math.min(cols - 1, Math.floor((sx + rad) / cw));
      const r0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((sy - rad) / ch));
      const r1 = Math.min(rows - 1, Math.floor((sy + rad) / ch));
      for (let row = r0; row <= r1; row++) {
        const dy = (row + 0.5) * ch - sy;
        for (let col = c0; col <= c1; col++) {
          const dx = (col + 0.5) * cw - sx;
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= rad * rad) g.fillRect(col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Pads, duckweed and then water lilies, drawn over the creatures. Every cell a pad covers is set in padMask,
   * so rings and duckweed skip it. koiMask marks cells held by a shallow koi: the pad leaves those cells alone,
   * so the koi shows on top. Lilies are drawn last, standing above the water.
   */
  drawPlants(
    g: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
    cw: number,
    ch: number,
    cols: number,
    rows: number,
    padMask: Uint8Array,
    koiMask: Uint8Array,
  ) {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.padCount; i++) {
      const cx = this.padX[i];
      const cy = this.padY[i];
      const rad = this.padR[i];
      const axis = this.padRot[i] + this.padNotch[i];
      const nx = Math.cos(axis);
      const ny = Math.sin(axis);
      const c0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((cx - rad) / cw));
      const c1 = Math.min(cols - 1, Math.floor((cx + rad) / cw));
      const r0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((cy - rad) / ch));
      const r1 = Math.min(rows - 1, Math.floor((cy + rad) / ch));
      // The cell holding the centre always counts as covered, so a tiny pad still shows.
      const ccol = Math.floor(cx / cw);
      const crow = Math.floor(cy / ch);
      for (let row = r0; row <= r1; row++) {
        for (let col = c0; col <= c1; col++) {
          const idx = row * cols + col;
          if (padMask[idx] || koiMask[idx]) continue;
          // Coverage: the sub-cell points inside the disc, minus the notch wedge.
          let hits = 0;
          for (let k = 0; k < SUB.length; k += 2) {
            const dx = (col + SUB[k]) * cw - cx;
            const dy = (row + SUB[k + 1]) * ch - cy;
            const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
            if (d > rad) continue;
            if (d > 0 && (dx * nx + dy * ny) / d >= NOTCH_COS) continue;
            hits++;
          }
          const centre = col === ccol && row === crow;
          if (hits === 0 && centre) hits = 1;
          if (hits === 0) continue;
          padMask[idx] = 1;
          const gx = col * cw + cw / 2;
          const gy = row * ch + ch / 2 + 1;
          const dx = (col + 0.5) * cw - cx;
          const dy = (row + 0.5) * ch - cy;
          const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          // Rim: the outermost ring, 1 to 3 sub-cell hits, or a neighbour outside the disc.
          const rim = hits < 4 || this.touchesEdge(col, row, cx, cy, rad, cw, ch);
          if (rim) {
            g.fillStyle = this.rimStyle;
            g.fillRect(col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch);
            if (!centre) {
              g.fillStyle = this.rimInkStyle;
              g.fillText(hash3(col, row, SALT + 1) < 0.5 ? "." : "'", gx, gy);
            }
          } else {
            // Body: three radial shades with a hash jitter of one step, so the leaf is not flat.
            const t = d / rad;
            let s = t < INNER_EDGE ? 0 : t < MID_EDGE ? 1 : 2;
            s = clamp(s + Math.floor(hash3(col, row, SALT + 2) * 3) - 1, 0, 2);
            g.fillStyle =
              s === 0 ? this.bodyInnerStyle : s === 1 ? this.bodyMidStyle : this.bodyOuterStyle;
            g.fillRect(col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch);
            if (!centre && this.onSpoke(i, dx, dy, d, cw)) {
              g.fillStyle = this.veinStyle;
              g.fillText(veinGlyph(dx, dy), gx, gy);
            }
          }
          if (centre) {
            // The stem attaches at the centre.
            g.fillStyle = this.veinStyle;
            g.fillText("o", gx, gy);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    for (let f = 0; f < this.frondCount; f++) {
      const p = this.frondPatch[f];
      this.drawFrond(
        g,
        this.patchX[p] + this.frondDx[f],
        this.patchY[p] + this.frondDy[f],
        this.frondGlyph[f],
        this.frondShade[f],
        cw,
        ch,
        cols,
        rows,
        padMask,
      );
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < this.looseCount; k++) {
      this.drawFrond(
        g,
        this.looseX[k],
        this.looseY[k],
        this.looseGlyph[k],
        this.looseShade[k],
        cw,
        ch,
        cols,
        rows,
        padMask,
      );
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < this.padCount; i++) {
      if (!this.padFlower[i]) continue;
      const rad = this.padR[i];
      const fa = this.padRot[i] + this.padFlowerAng[i];
      const fc = Math.floor((this.padX[i] + Math.cos(fa) * FLOWER_REACH * rad) / cw);
      const fr = Math.floor((this.padY[i] + Math.sin(fa) * FLOWER_REACH * rad) / ch);
      const petal = this.padFlowerWhite[i] ? this.whiteStyle : this.pinkStyle;
      for (let dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) {
        for (let dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
          const col = fc + dc;
          const row = fr + dr;
          if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= cols || row >= rows) continue;
          const glyph = FLOWER_ROWS[dr + 1][dc + 1];
          g.fillStyle = glyph === "@" ? this.stamenStyle : petal;
          g.fillText(glyph, col * cw + cw / 2, row * ch + ch / 2 + 1);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /** One frond: a single glyph in one of the two greens, unless a pad covers its cell. */
  private drawFrond(
    g: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
    x: number,
    y: number,
    glyph: number,
    shade: number,
    cw: number,
    ch: number,
    cols: number,
    rows: number,
    padMask: Uint8Array,
  ) {
    const col = Math.floor(x / cw);
    const row = Math.floor(y / ch);
    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= cols || row >= rows) return;
    if (padMask[row * cols + col]) return;
    g.fillStyle = shade === 0 ? this.duckStyle : this.duckDarkStyle;
    g.fillText(glyph === 0 ? "°" : "·", col * cw + cw / 2, row * ch + ch / 2 + 1);
  }

  /** True when the cell's offset (dx, dy) from pad i's centre lies on one of its veins. */
  private onSpoke(i: number, dx: number, dy: number, d: number, cw: number) {
    if (d <= VEIN_INNER * this.padR[i]) return false;
    const n = this.padSpokeN[i];
    const step = TAU / n;
    const axis = this.padNotch[i];
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const a = this.padSpokeBase[i] + k * step;
      // Spokes near the notch are skipped, so the notch reads as a clean cut.
      if (Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(a - axis), Math.cos(a - axis))) < SPOKE_NOTCH_CLEAR)
        continue;
      // A ray from the centre: the cell must lie ahead of the centre along the spoke.
      if (dx * Math.cos(a) + dy * Math.sin(a) <= 0) continue;
      if (Math.abs(dx * Math.sin(a) - dy * Math.cos(a)) < VEIN_HALF * cw) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /** True when one of the cell's four neighbours has its centre outside the pad's disc. */
  private touchesEdge(
    col: number,
    row: number,
    cx: number,
    cy: number,
    rad: number,
    cw: number,
    ch: number,
  ) {
    return (
      outsideDisc(col - 1, row, cx, cy, rad, cw, ch) ||
      outsideDisc(col + 1, row, cx, cy, rad, cw, ch) ||
      outsideDisc(col, row - 1, cx, cy, rad, cw, ch) ||
      outsideDisc(col, row + 1, cx, cy, rad, cw, ch)
    );
  }

  /** A seeded draw in [0, 1): the same canvas size always draws the same sequence. */
  private rnd() {
    return hash3(this.cursor++, this.seed, SALT);
  }

  private overlapsPad(x: number, y: number, r: number) {
    for (let j = 0; j < this.padCount; j++) {
      if (Math.hypot(x - this.padX[j], y - this.padY[j]) < r + this.padR[j]) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  private placePad(i: number, x: number, y: number, r: number) {
    this.padX[i] = x;
    this.padY[i] = y;
    this.padR[i] = r;
    this.padRot[i] = this.rnd() * TAU;
    this.padSpin[i] = (this.rnd() * 2 - 1) * PAD_SPIN;
    this.padNotch[i] = this.rnd() * TAU;
    // The lily sits on the far side of the notch, so the notch never covers it.
    this.padFlowerAng[i] = this.padNotch[i] + Math.PI;
    this.padSpokeBase[i] = this.rnd() * TAU;
    this.padSpokeN[i] = SPOKES_MIN + Math.floor(this.rnd() * (SPOKES_MAX - SPOKES_MIN + 1));
    this.padFlower[i] = this.rnd() < FLOWER_CHANCE ? 1 : 0;
    this.padFlowerWhite[i] = this.rnd() < FLOWER_WHITE_CHANCE ? 1 : 0;
    this.padVx[i] = 0;
    this.padVy[i] = 0;
  }
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/sea.ts
```ts
import { hash3, smoothstep, TAU } from "./math";

/**
 * The open-sea surface, seen from above. A directional sum of sines (a Tessendorf-style sea, with
 * a Phillips-like spectrum) gives the height and its slope along the sun. From those come the swell
 * shading, the sun glitter (Cox and Munk), the wind streaks and the whitecaps.
 *
 * Allocation-free after layout: every per-cell and per-patch array is sized once there.
 */

/** Wave components in the swell sum. */
const COMPONENTS = 14;
/** Shortest and longest wavelengths (px), log-spaced, each with a little hash jitter. */
const LAMBDA_MIN = 70;
const LAMBDA_MAX = 520;
const LAMBDA_JITTER = 0.15;
/** The longest wave has this period (s); it sets g in ω = √(g·2π/λ). */
const LONGEST_PERIOD = 9;
/** Directions spread about the wind by a cos^4 law, within ±45°. */
const SPREAD_MAX = Math.PI / 4;
const SPREAD_POWER = 4;
/** Unit vector from the water toward the sun: the sun is up and to the left. */
const SUN_X = -0.6;
const SUN_Y = -0.8;

/** Glitter: a facet sparkles when its normalised slope is near GLINT_S0, within GLINT_SIGMA. */
const GLINT_S0 = 1.0;
const GLINT_SIGMA = 0.7;
const GLINT_GAIN = 0.12;
/** Cells whose band weight is below this never sparkle (their chance is negligible). */
const GLINT_MIN_BAND = 0.02;
/** A sparkle is re-rolled this many times a second, so it lasts one tick. */
export const GLINT_TICK = 9;
/** The glitter band's centre, as a fraction of the canvas from its top-left, off-canvas toward the sun. */
const BAND_X = -0.35;
const BAND_Y = -0.45;
/** Band falloff, as a fraction of the canvas diagonal: long along the wind, narrow across it. */
const BAND_ALONG = 0.65;
const BAND_ACROSS = 0.36;

/** Windrows: 3 to 6 lines, a sway of this many px across the wind, and a drift of this many px/s downwind. */
const STREAK_MIN = 3;
const STREAK_RANGE = 4;
const STREAK_SWAY = 12;
const STREAK_SPEED = 6;
/** Along-line noise lattice spacing (px): dashes run about this long. */
const STREAK_LENGTH = 36;
/** A streak cell shows where its noise is above this; above STREAK_STRONG it is the brighter level. */
const STREAK_ON = 0.55;
const STREAK_STRONG = 0.75;

/** Whitecaps: a fixed pool of patches, at most one new patch per WHITECAP_GATE seconds. */
const WHITECAP_POOL = 12;
const WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS = 10;
const WHITECAP_MIN_CELLS = 4;
const WHITECAP_GATE = 2;
const WHITECAP_LIFE = 4;
const WHITECAP_LIFE_RANGE = 2;
const WHITECAP_SPEED = 8;
/** Top 0.5% of a Gaussian sits at about 2.58 standard deviations. */
const WHITECAP_SIGMAS = 2.58;
/** Patch cells fall within this many cells of the patch centre. */
const WHITECAP_RADIUS = 1.6;

/** Glyphs for whitecap foam, keyed by foamGlyph: 1 is `.`, 2 is `,`, 3 is `°`. */
export const FOAM_GLYPHS = ["", ".", ",", "°"] as const;

export class SeaSurface {
  private cols = 0;
  private rows = 0;
  private cw = 12;
  private ch = 18;
  private n = 0;
  private seed = 0;
  /** Whitecaps spawned so far: varies the hash that places each new patch. */
  private caps = 0;

  /** Wave components: wavenumber × cos/sin of the direction, the amplitude and the phase. */
  private amp = new Float32Array(COMPONENTS);
  private slopeAmp = new Float32Array(COMPONENTS);
  private omega = new Float64Array(COMPONENTS);
  private rowPhase0 = new Float64Array(0);
  private colS = new Float32Array(0);
  private colC = new Float32Array(0);
  private rowS = new Float32Array(0);
  private rowC = new Float32Array(0);
  private sigmaSlope = 1;
  private sigmaHeight = 1;

  /** Wind direction (unit vector), the way the swell and the streaks run. */
  windX = 1;
  windY = 0;

  /** Per cell: the swell height, and the slope along the sun (in units of its RMS). */
  height = new Float32Array(0);
  z = new Float32Array(0);
  /** Per cell: the chance of a sparkle this tick, from the slope and the band. */
  glint = new Float32Array(0);
  /** Per cell: the glitter band's weight, 0 to 1. */
  band = new Float32Array(0);
  /** Per cell: 0 for none, 1 for a dim windrow cell, 2 for a bright one. */
  streak = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Per cell: a foam glyph index into FOAM_GLYPHS, 0 for none, and an alpha level 0 to 2. */
  foamGlyph = new Uint8Array(0);
  foamLevel = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Per cell, static: the cell centre measured across and along the wind, from the canvas centre. */
  private across = new Float32Array(0);
  private along = new Float32Array(0);
  private laneOffset = new Float32Array(STREAK_RANGE + STREAK_MIN);
  private streaks = 0;

  /** Whitecap pool: centre (px), age and life (s), cell count, and per-cell offsets and glyphs. */
  private wcActive = new Uint8Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcX = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcY = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcAge = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcLife = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcCount = new Uint8Array(WHITECAP_POOL);
  private wcOffX = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL * WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS);
  private wcOffY = new Float32Array(WHITECAP_POOL * WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS);
  private wcGlyph = new Uint8Array(WHITECAP_POOL * WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS);
  private wcGate = 0;
  private time = 0;

  /**
   * Sizes every array for a cols × rows grid of cw × ch cells, and draws the swell from `seed`. Call
   * once per resize; the same seed gives the same sea.
   */
  layout(cols: number, rows: number, cw: number, ch: number, seed: number) {
    this.cols = cols;
    this.rows = rows;
    this.cw = cw;
    this.ch = ch;
    this.n = cols * rows;
    this.seed = seed;
    const K = COMPONENTS;
    const W = cols * cw;
    const H = rows * ch;
    const diag = Math.hypot(W, H);

    const theta = hash3(seed, 1, 101) * TAU;
    this.windX = Math.cos(theta);
    this.windY = Math.sin(theta);
    const sunLen = Math.hypot(SUN_X, SUN_Y);
    const sx = SUN_X / sunLen;
    const sy = SUN_Y / sunLen;
    const g = ((TAU / LONGEST_PERIOD) ** 2 * LAMBDA_MAX) / TAU;

    // Pass 1: each component's direction, wavelength and phase; then its raw amplitude.
    const rawAmp = new Float64Array(K);
    const dirCos = new Float64Array(K);
    const dirSin = new Float64Array(K);
    const wavePhase = new Float64Array(K);
    const waveK = new Float64Array(K);
    let ampSum = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const base = LAMBDA_MIN * (LAMBDA_MAX / LAMBDA_MIN) ** (k / (K - 1));
      const lambda = base * (1 + LAMBDA_JITTER * (2 * hash3(seed, k, 2) - 1));
      // Rejection-sample the spread from cos^4 over ±45°; a run of misses falls back to the wind.
      let spread = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
        const s = (2 * hash3(seed, k, 10 + j) - 1) * SPREAD_MAX;
        if (hash3(seed, k, 40 + j) < Math.cos(s) ** SPREAD_POWER) {
          spread = s;
          break;
        }
      }
      const dir = theta + spread;
      const kw = TAU / lambda;
      waveK[k] = kw;
      dirCos[k] = Math.cos(dir);
      dirSin[k] = Math.sin(dir);
      wavePhase[k] = hash3(seed, k, 3) * TAU;
      this.omega[k] = Math.sqrt((g * TAU) / lambda);
      // Phillips-like: the amplitude grows with wavelength and sits along the wind.
      rawAmp[k] = lambda ** 1.5 * Math.cos(spread) ** 2;
      ampSum += rawAmp[k];
    }

    let sumAmp2 = 0;
    let sumSlope2 = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
      const a = rawAmp[k] / ampSum;
      this.amp[k] = a;
      this.slopeAmp[k] = a * waveK[k] * (dirCos[k] * sx + dirSin[k] * sy);
      sumAmp2 += this.amp[k] ** 2;
      sumSlope2 += this.slopeAmp[k] ** 2;
    }
    // The RMS of a sum of random-phase sines is the root of half the sum of squares.
    this.sigmaHeight = Math.sqrt(sumAmp2 / 2);
    this.sigmaSlope = Math.sqrt(sumSlope2 / 2) || 1;

    // Static per-column and per-row tables: the x and y parts of each component's phase.
    this.colS = new Float32Array(cols * K);
    this.colC = new Float32Array(cols * K);
    this.rowPhase0 = new Float64Array(rows * K);
    this.rowS = new Float32Array(rows * K);
    this.rowC = new Float32Array(rows * K);
    for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
      const x = (c + 0.5) * cw;
      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
        const p = waveK[k] * x * dirCos[k];
        this.colS[c * K + k] = Math.sin(p);
        this.colC[c * K + k] = Math.cos(p);
      }
    }
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const y = (r + 0.5) * ch;
      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
        this.rowPhase0[r * K + k] = waveK[k] * y * dirSin[k] + wavePhase[k];
      }
    }

    // Per-cell statics: the glitter band, and the streak frame (across and along the wind).
    this.height = new Float32Array(this.n);
    this.z = new Float32Array(this.n);
    this.glint = new Float32Array(this.n);
    this.band = new Float32Array(this.n);
    this.streak = new Uint8Array(this.n);
    this.foamGlyph = new Uint8Array(this.n);
    this.foamLevel = new Uint8Array(this.n);
    this.across = new Float32Array(this.n);
    this.along = new Float32Array(this.n);
    const bandX = BAND_X * W;
    const bandY = BAND_Y * H;
    const la = BAND_ALONG * diag;
    const lb = BAND_ACROSS * diag;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        const i = r * cols + c;
        const x = (c + 0.5) * cw;
        const y = (r + 0.5) * ch;
        const dx = x - bandX;
        const dy = y - bandY;
        const bAlong = dx * this.windX + dy * this.windY;
        const bAcross = -dx * this.windY + dy * this.windX;
        this.band[i] = Math.exp(-(bAlong ** 2) / (2 * la * la) - bAcross ** 2 / (2 * lb * lb));
        const ox = x - W / 2;
        const oy = y - H / 2;
        this.along[i] = ox * this.windX + oy * this.windY;
        this.across[i] = -ox * this.windY + oy * this.windX;
      }
    }

    // Windrows: 3 to 6 lanes, spaced irregularly across the wind.
    this.streaks = STREAK_MIN + Math.floor(hash3(seed, 2, 60) * STREAK_RANGE);
    for (let k = 0; k < this.streaks; k++) {
      this.laneOffset[k] = (hash3(seed, k, 61) - 0.5) * 0.9 * diag;
    }

    this.wcActive.fill(0);
    this.wcGate = WHITECAP_GATE;
    this.time = 0;
    this.caps = 0;
  }

  /** Advances the sea to time t (s). Call once per rendered frame, natural water only. */
  step(t: number, dt: number) {
    const { cols, rows, colS, colC, rowS, rowC, rowPhase0, omega, amp, slopeAmp } = this;
    const K = COMPONENTS;
    this.time = t;
    // The time term is per row and component, so each cell only adds its column and row parts.
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
        const p = rowPhase0[r * K + k] - omega[k] * t;
        rowS[r * K + k] = Math.sin(p);
        rowC[r * K + k] = Math.cos(p);
      }
    }
    const { height, z, glint, band } = this;
    const invSlope = 1 / this.sigmaSlope;
    for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
      const rb = r * K;
      for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {
        const cb = c * K;
        let h = 0;
        let s = 0;
        for (let k = 0; k < K; k++) {
          const cs = colS[cb + k];
          const cc = colC[cb + k];
          const rs = rowS[rb + k];
          const rc = rowC[rb + k];
          // Angle-sum identities: sin(a + b) and cos(a + b) from the column and row parts.
          h += amp[k] * (cs * rc + cc * rs);
          s += slopeAmp[k] * (cc * rc - cs * rs);
        }
        const i = r * cols + c;
        height[i] = h;
        z[i] = s * invSlope;
      }
    }

    // Glitter: a facet sparkles where its slope reflects the sun, and the band makes that happen mostly in one region.
    for (let i = 0; i < this.n; i++) {
      // Outside the band the weight is near zero, so skip the exp there.
      const b = band[i];
      if (b < GLINT_MIN_BAND) {
        glint[i] = 0;
        continue;
      }
      const d = (z[i] - GLINT_S0) / GLINT_SIGMA;
      glint[i] = GLINT_GAIN * Math.exp(-0.5 * d * d) * b * b;
    }

    this.updateStreaks(t);
    this.updateWhitecaps(dt);
  }

  /** The glitter band's weight at a cell, 0 to 1. */
  bandAt(c: number, r: number) {
    return this.band[r * this.cols + c];
  }

  /** The sparkle roll for a cell on this tick: GLINT_TICK ticks a second, so a sparkle lasts one tick. */
  sparkle(c: number, r: number, t: number) {
    const tick = Math.floor(t * GLINT_TICK);
    return hash3(c, r, tick) < this.glint[r * this.cols + c];
  }

  private updateStreaks(t: number) {
    const { streak, across, along, cw, n } = this;
    streak.fill(0);
    for (let k = 0; k < this.streaks; k++) {
      // Each lane wanders across the wind, and its noise runs downwind so the dashes travel.
      const lane = this.laneOffset[k] + STREAK_SWAY * Math.sin(0.05 * t + k);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        if (Math.abs(across[i] - lane) > 0.7 * cw) continue;
        const v = valueNoise((along[i] - STREAK_SPEED * t) / STREAK_LENGTH, k, this.seed);
        if (v <= STREAK_ON) continue;
        const level = v > STREAK_STRONG ? 2 : 1;
        if (level > streak[i]) streak[i] = level;
      }
    }
  }

  private updateWhitecaps(dt: number) {
    const { wcActive, wcX, wcY, wcAge, wcLife, wcCount, wcOffX, wcOffY, wcGlyph } = this;
    const { foamGlyph, foamLevel, cw, ch, cols, rows, height, n } = this;
    foamGlyph.fill(0);
    foamLevel.fill(0);
    const dx = this.windX * WHITECAP_SPEED * dt;
    const dy = this.windY * WHITECAP_SPEED * dt;
    for (let p = 0; p < WHITECAP_POOL; p++) {
      if (!wcActive[p]) continue;
      wcAge[p] += dt;
      if (wcAge[p] >= wcLife[p]) {
        wcActive[p] = 0;
        continue;
      }
      wcX[p] += dx;
      wcY[p] += dy;
      const age01 = wcAge[p] / wcLife[p];
      const level = age01 < 0.5 ? 2 : age01 < 0.8 ? 1 : 0;
      for (let m = 0; m < wcCount[p]; m++) {
        const k = p * WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS + m;
        const c = Math.floor((wcX[p] + wcOffX[k]) / cw);
        const r = Math.floor((wcY[p] + wcOffY[k]) / ch);
        if (c < 0 || r < 0 || c >= cols || r >= rows) continue;
        const i = r * cols + c;
        if (foamGlyph[i]) continue;
        foamGlyph[i] = wcGlyph[k];
        foamLevel[i] = level;
      }
    }

    this.wcGate -= dt;
    if (this.wcGate > 0) return;
    // Find a free slot and a cell on a crest. The scan starts at a hashed cell so the spot changes.
    let slot = -1;
    for (let p = 0; p < WHITECAP_POOL; p++) {
      if (!wcActive[p]) {
        slot = p;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (slot < 0) return;
    const thr = WHITECAP_SIGMAS * this.sigmaHeight;
    const start = Math.floor(hash3(this.seed, Math.floor(this.time * 10), 70) * n);
    let crest = -1;
    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
      const i = (start + j) % n;
      if (height[i] > thr) {
        crest = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (crest < 0) return;
    this.wcGate = WHITECAP_GATE + hash3(this.seed, this.caps, 71);
    this.caps++;
    const cx = ((crest % cols) + 0.5) * cw;
    const cy = (Math.floor(crest / cols) + 0.5) * ch;
    wcActive[slot] = 1;
    wcX[slot] = cx;
    wcY[slot] = cy;
    wcAge[slot] = 0;
    wcLife[slot] = WHITECAP_LIFE + WHITECAP_LIFE_RANGE * hash3(this.seed, this.caps, 72);
    const count =
      WHITECAP_MIN_CELLS +
      Math.floor(hash3(this.seed, this.caps, 73) * (WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS - WHITECAP_MIN_CELLS + 1));
    wcCount[slot] = count;
    for (let m = 0; m < count; m++) {
      const k = slot * WHITECAP_MAX_CELLS + m;
      wcOffX[k] = (hash3(this.seed, this.caps * 16 + m, 74) * 2 - 1) * WHITECAP_RADIUS * cw;
      wcOffY[k] = (hash3(this.seed, this.caps * 16 + m, 75) * 2 - 1) * WHITECAP_RADIUS * ch;
      const pick = hash3(this.seed, this.caps * 16 + m, 76);
      wcGlyph[k] = pick < 0.4 ? 3 : pick < 0.7 ? 1 : 2;
    }
  }
}

/** One-dimensional value noise in [0, 1): lattice values from the hash, smoothly blended. */
function valueNoise(u: number, lane: number, seed: number) {
  const i = Math.floor(u);
  const f = u - i;
  const a = hash3(i, lane, seed + 17);
  const b = hash3(i + 1, lane, seed + 17);
  return a + (b - a) * smoothstep(0, 1, f);
}
```

## packages/core/src/components/ascii-ocean/renderer.ts
```ts
// Ported from https://github.com/stackedlol/ascii-sharks (used with the author's permission).
/**
 * Draws the World as a grid of ASCII cells:
 *   1. a cell grid on the page background, in the page's colours
 *   2. a wake field that diffuses where things swim, lighting the dots cyan
 *   3. fish as minimal ASCII dashes, one per cell, glinting on hard turns
 *   4. creatures rasterised cell-by-cell with supersampled edges: grey blocks
 *      shaded by the light, dense glyphs, and shimmering accent colours
 */
import { clamp, hash3, oklchToRgb, rgbToOklabL, smoothstep, TAU } from "./math";
import { CREATURE_IDS, CREATURES } from "./creatures";
import type {
  Coat,
  CreatureDef,
  OceanColors,
  OceanScene,
  PaintPoint,
  TailShape,
} from "./creatures/types";
import { Effects, type ParticleKind } from "./effects";
import { PondSurface } from "./pond";
import { FOAM_GLYPHS, GLINT_TICK, SeaSurface } from "./sea";
import { type Creature, SPINE_NODES, type World } from "./world";

export type OceanPalette = {
  /** Page background, opaque rgb 0–255. */
  background: [number, number, number];
  /** Page foreground (text colour), rgb 0–255. */
  foreground: [number, number, number];
  /** "light" when the background is light; greys are inverted so the scene reads as a negative. */
  mode: "light" | "dark";
};

/** Used until the engine reads the page theme: the original black-background look. */
export const DEFAULT_PALETTE: OceanPalette = {
  background: [0, 0, 0],
  foreground: [230, 238, 255],
  mode: "dark",
};

const ALPHA_LEVELS = 6;
const FISH_ALPHAS = [0.28, 0.42, 0.58, 0.74, 0.9];

// Dot colour classes: blue, green, red, pale; the last entry is the cyan wake.
const DOT_RGB: [number, number, number][] = [
  [48, 52, 235],
  [52, 205, 60],
  [228, 50, 44],
  [190, 212, 255],
  [110, 230, 255],
];
const DOT_BASE_ALPHA = [1, 1, 1, 1, 1];
const WAKE = DOT_RGB.length - 1;
// The pale cyan wake washes out on light backgrounds, so it darkens there.
const WAKE_RGB_LIGHT: [number, number, number] = [0, 130, 180];

/** Food colours by scene (ocean, pond) for dark and light pages; light pages get darker variants. */
const MORSEL_RGB = {
  dark: [
    [255, 170, 90],
    [214, 190, 140],
  ],
  light: [
    [200, 110, 40],
    [150, 120, 70],
  ],
} as const;
const MORSEL_GLYPHS = ["*", "o"];
/** Food alpha is quantised to this many steps, each with its own preallocated style. */
const MORSEL_LEVELS = 5;
const MORSEL_ALPHA = 0.95;

// Water surface (natural mode, surface on, ocean and pond): the open sea from above, built in sea.ts.
/** Swell tint: light on a face that tilts toward the sun, shade on one that tilts away. */
const SWELL_RGB = { dark: [170, 225, 255], light: [0, 110, 160] } as const;
const SWELL_SHADE_RGB = [0, 0, 0] as const;
/** Peak alpha of the swell tints per page mode; the top level of each is exactly this. */
const SWELL_PEAK = {
  dark: { lit: 0.025, shade: 0.03 },
  light: { lit: 0.02, shade: 0.02 },
} as const;
const SWELL_LEVELS = 4;
/** Normalised slope where the tint starts, and the span over which it reaches the top level. */
const SWELL_START = 0.8;
const SWELL_SPAN = 1.6;
/** Glitter sparkle colour per page mode, and its alpha per brightness level. */
const GLINT_RGB = { dark: [255, 244, 214], light: [20, 70, 120] } as const;
const GLINT_ALPHA = { dark: [0.35, 0.5, 0.7], light: [0.4, 0.5, 0.6] } as const;
/** One sparkle in this many is a `*`, on dark pages only (a `*` does not read on light ones). */
const GLINT_STAR = 40;
/** Windrow and whitecap foam colour per page mode. Windrows have two alpha levels, foam three. */
const FOAM_RGB = { dark: [225, 240, 250], light: [30, 60, 80] } as const;
const STREAK_ALPHA = [0.09, 0.13] as const;
const FOAM_ALPHA = [0.25, 0.42, 0.6] as const;

// Surface rings on natural water: a wobbling radius, gaps in the arc, and the swell's drift (px/s).
// The still pond wobbles less and has fewer gaps. Ring glyphs are dots, ticks and commas only.
const RING_WOBBLE = 0.18;
const RING_GAP = 0.45;
const RING_WOBBLE_POND = 0.06;
const RING_GAP_POND = 0.27;
const RING_DRIFT = 6;
const RING_GLYPHS = [".", "'", "`", ","];
/** A bite's ring set lasts this long, in seconds, from its start to the outer ring's edge. */
const RIPPLE_LIFE = 0.8;
/** The sea's layout seed: one fixed sea, the same on every load. */
const SEA_SEED = 0x5ea;

/** Phase of harmonic `j` of a ring with shape seed `seed` (0 to 1): each harmonic gets its own offset. */
function ringPhase(seed: number, j: number) {
  return TAU * ((seed * (7.31 + 3.7 * j)) % 1);
}

/** `levels` alpha steps up to `peak`: step k is (k + 1) / levels of it, so the top step is the peak. */
function rampStyles(rgb: readonly number[], peak: number, levels: number) {
  return Array.from(
    { length: levels },
    (_, k) => `rgba(${rgb.join(",")},${((peak * (k + 1)) / levels).toFixed(3)})`,
  );
}

// Rings and particles: each alpha quantised to ALPHA_LEVELS styles per kind.
const SPRAY_RGB = { dark: [230, 238, 255], light: [60, 70, 85] } as const;
const BUBBLE_RGB = { dark: [190, 240, 255], light: [0, 120, 160] } as const;
const PARTICLE_ALPHA = 0.85;
/** Blow mist is 1.6× the particle alpha, capped so it stays a soft cloud. */
const MIST_ALPHA = Math.min(0.9, PARTICLE_ALPHA * 1.6);

const ACCENTS = ["#e0892c", "#e03a32", "#b44ae0", "#33c8cc", "#3cb54a", "#3150e8", "#d9c14a"];

/** Fixed creature glyph colours: the dark-mode values, and their inverses for light mode. */
const INK = {
  dark: { bright: "#ffffff", ink: "#0e0f13", inkSoft: "#1b1d24", eye: "#9aa0ad" },
  light: { bright: "#000000", ink: "#f1f0ec", inkSoft: "#e4e2db", eye: "#655f52" },
};

// Sparse glyphs on bright skin, dense glyphs in shadow, so the texture
// reinforces the shading instead of fighting it.
const SKIN_RAMP = ":;t%SX8#@";
const FIN_GLYPHS = "%;tS:";
const RIDGE_GLYPHS = "@8%";
const EDGE_HEAVY = ":;";
const EDGE_LIGHT = ".',`";

const FLAG_BODY = 1;
const FLAG_EDGE = 2;
const FLAG_FIN = 3;
const FLAG_RIDGE = 4;
const FLAG_EYE = 5;
/** Dark skin markings: tiger bars, zebra-shark spots. */
const FLAG_MARK = 6;
const MARK_GLYPHS = "#%@";

// Sub-cell sample offsets for anti-aliased silhouettes.
const SUBSAMPLES = [0.25, 0.3, 0.75, 0.3, 0.25, 0.72, 0.75, 0.72];

// Natural mode only. Five bits per channel for the fillStyle cache.
const STYLE_CACHE_SIZE = 1 << 15;
/**
 * Beer-Lambert absorption rate per channel, in 1/m: light fades as exp(-k · z) over the water it crosses.
 * Red goes first and blue survives longest in clear ocean water.
 */
const WATER_K = [0.9, 0.35, 0.18];
/**
 * Pond water is tannin-brown and algae-green, so it absorbs blue more than green: r > b > g.
 * Deep pond water reads green-brown rather than blue.
 */
const POND_WATER_K = [0.75, 0.32, 0.5];
// Veil: a uniform haze of the page colour over everything at depth, the same for all channels.
const VEIL_K = 0.35;
const DEPTH_STEPS = 16;

/** Factor per depth step (depth quantised to 1/16), so no Math.exp per cell. z = (1 - depth) * 2.2. */
function waterTable(k: number) {
  const table = new Float32Array(DEPTH_STEPS + 1);
  for (let i = 0; i <= DEPTH_STEPS; i++) {
    table[i] = Math.exp(-k * (1 - i / DEPTH_STEPS) * 2.2);
  }
  return table;
}

type WaterTables = { r: Float32Array; g: Float32Array; b: Float32Array; veil: Float32Array };

/** Per-channel absorb factors (light from the animal loses red first) and the shared veil, for one scene's water. */
function waterTables(k: readonly number[], veilK: number): WaterTables {
  return {
    r: waterTable(k[0]),
    g: waterTable(k[1]),
    b: waterTable(k[2]),
    veil: waterTable(veilK),
  };
}

const OCEAN_WATER = waterTables(WATER_K, VEIL_K);
// Garden ponds are shallow: less veil than the open sea, so koi keep their colours at depth.
const POND_WATER = waterTables(POND_WATER_K, 0.25);

function depthIndex(depth: number) {
  return clamp(Math.round(depth * DEPTH_STEPS), 0, DEPTH_STEPS);
}

/** Absorb the channel, then veil it toward the page background. */
function attenuateChannel(c: number, bg: number, absorb: number, haze: number) {
  return clamp(Math.round(bg + (c * absorb - bg) * haze), 0, 255);
}

/**
 * Natural colour as seen through water at depth (0 far, 1 near): absorbed, then veiled toward bg.
 * The scene picks the water; the ocean default is the original behaviour.
 */
export function attenuate(
  c: [number, number, number],
  bg: [number, number, number],
  depth: number,
  scene: OceanScene = "ocean",
): [number, number, number] {
  const w = scene === "pond" ? POND_WATER : OCEAN_WATER;
  const zi = depthIndex(depth);
  return [
    attenuateChannel(c[0], bg[0], w.r[zi], w.veil[zi]),
    attenuateChannel(c[1], bg[1], w.g[zi], w.veil[zi]),
    attenuateChannel(c[2], bg[2], w.b[zi], w.veil[zi]),
  ];
}

// Coat ramps: lighting steps per region, as rgb before water. Built once per coat, then only looked up.
const COAT_STEPS = 16;
// Koi fins are translucent: they veil as if one step further off.
const COAT_FIN_VEIL = 0.15;
// Glyph ink on a coat's dark regions: a light ink, so black patches still show texture.
const COAT_LIGHT_INK = "rgba(236,238,242,0.55)";
// Glint glyphs on a coat with `glint`: pale gold or white.
const COAT_GLINT = ["#f6e7a8", "#ffffff"];
// Lightness margin from the page: coat regions stay this far off the background's OKLab lightness.
const COAT_CONTRAST_DARK = 0.12;
const COAT_CONTRAST_LIGHT = 0.06;

/** Per region, COAT_STEPS rgb triples: the region's OKLCH lit from dark to bright, kept off the page. */
function buildCoatRamps(coat: Coat, bgL: number, dark: boolean): Uint8Array[] {
  return coat.regions.map(([l, c, h]) => {
    const ramp = new Uint8Array(COAT_STEPS * 3);
    for (let k = 0; k < COAT_STEPS; k++) {
      const shade = k / (COAT_STEPS - 1);
      let lit = clamp(l * (0.62 + 0.55 * shade), 0, 1);
      lit = clamp(
        dark ? Math.max(lit, bgL + COAT_CONTRAST_DARK) : Math.min(lit, bgL - COAT_CONTRAST_LIGHT),
        0,
        1,
      );
      const [r, g, b] = oklchToRgb(lit, c, h);
      ramp[3 * k] = r;
      ramp[3 * k + 1] = g;
      ramp[3 * k + 2] = b;
    }
    return ramp;
  });
}

/** Natural body fill level for a half cell, by its flag. */
function halfLevel(flag: number, shade: number) {
  if (flag === FLAG_RIDGE) return 226;
  if (flag === FLAG_EYE) return 12;
  if (flag === FLAG_MARK) return 24 + shade * 22;
  if (flag === FLAG_FIN) return 40 + shade * 120;
  return 46 + shade * 160;
}

/** How far a tail reaches across the body, as a fraction of length: a fluke's span or a caudal fin's widest half-width. */
function tailSpan(tail: TailShape) {
  return tail.kind === "fluke" ? tail.span : tail.width + tail.twist;
}

/**
 * Rounded paddle fin: an ellipse from the middle of its root (r0–r1 along the body) out to the
 * tip at (tipU, reach). Its width is the root's length, so the blade stays broad to a round end.
 */
function inPaddle(u: number, a: number, r0: number, r1: number, tipU: number, reach: number) {
  const mu = (r0 + r1) / 2;
  const du = tipU - mu;
  const len = Math.hypot(du, reach);
  if (len === 0) return false;
  const along = ((u - mu) * du + a * reach) / len;
  const off = ((u - mu) * reach - a * du) / len;
  const half = len / 2;
  const width = Math.max((r1 - r0) / 2, 1);
  return ((along - half) / half) ** 2 + (off / width) ** 2 <= 1;
}

function inTriangle(
  px: number,
  py: number,
  ax: number,
  ay: number,
  bx: number,
  by: number,
  cx: number,
  cy: number,
) {
  const d1 = (px - bx) * (ay - by) - (ax - bx) * (py - by);
  const d2 = (px - cx) * (by - cy) - (bx - cx) * (py - cy);
  const d3 = (px - ax) * (cy - ay) - (cx - ax) * (py - ay);
  const neg = d1 < 0 || d2 < 0 || d3 < 0;
  const pos = d1 > 0 || d2 > 0 || d3 > 0;
  return !(neg && pos);
}

type Oklch = [number, number, number];

// The seabed, natural mode. It is the deepest layer: colours go through the water at the far depth, then a further veil toward the page.
const FLOOR_DEPTH = 0;
const FLOOR_VEIL = 0.45;
/** Low-frequency patches are value noise on a coarse lattice of this many columns and rows over the page. */
const FLOOR_NOISE_COLS = 12;
const FLOOR_NOISE_ROWS = 8;
const FLOOR_SALT = 0x5eab;
// Ocean: pale sand, long regular ripples, a few rocks and shells.
const SAND: Oklch = [0.8, 0.05, 85];
const RIPPLE_LAMBDA = 3.2;
const RIPPLE_THETA = 0.35;
const RIPPLE_RIDGE = 0.8;
const ROCK: Oklch = [0.45, 0.02, 60];
const SHELL: Oklch = [0.85, 0.03, 70];
// Pond: dark silt with rounded pebbles in grey, tan and rust, and sunken brown leaves.
const SILT: Oklch = [0.32, 0.04, 80];
const PEBBLES: Oklch[] = [
  [0.55, 0.01, 250],
  [0.6, 0.04, 75],
  [0.5, 0.07, 45],
];
const LEAF: Oklch = [0.4, 0.06, 60];

/** Value noise in 0–1: bilinear over hashes on a coarse lattice, so the patches span many cells. */
function floorNoise(col: number, row: number, cols: number, rows: number, z: number) {
  const gx = (col / cols) * FLOOR_NOISE_COLS;
  const gy = (row / rows) * FLOOR_NOISE_ROWS;
  const x0 = Math.floor(gx);
  const y0 = Math.floor(gy);
  const fx = gx - x0;
  const fy = gy - y0;
  const top = hash3(x0, y0, z) * (1 - fx) + hash3(x0 + 1, y0, z) * fx;
  const bottom = hash3(x0, y0 + 1, z) * (1 - fx) + hash3(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, z) * fx;
  return top * (1 - fy) + bottom * fy;
}

/** Cell grid, snapped to device pixels, stroked in `stroke` (a CSS colour). */
export function paintGrid(
  g: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  W: number,
  H: number,
  cw: number,
  ch: number,
  dpr: number,
  stroke: string,
) {
  g.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  g.strokeStyle = stroke;
  g.lineWidth = 1;
  g.beginPath();
  for (let c = 0; c <= Math.ceil(W / cw); c++) {
    const x = Math.round(c * cw * dpr) + 0.5;
    g.moveTo(x, 0);
    g.lineTo(x, H * dpr);
  }
  for (let r = 0; r <= Math.ceil(H / ch); r++) {
    const y = Math.round(r * ch * dpr) + 0.5;
    g.moveTo(0, y);
    g.lineTo(W * dpr, y);
  }
  g.stroke();
}

/** The depth a body is seen at: a diving cetacean (dive 1) reads 45% deeper, dimmer and bluer. Sharks never dive, so theirs is unchanged. */
function bodyDepthOf(sh: Creature) {
  return sh.dive === 0 ? sh.depth : sh.depth * (1 - 0.45 * sh.dive);
}

/**
 * The fraction of a body point at s (0 snout → 1 tail) that is out of the water, for Creature.emerge e. Rising
 * (e ≤ 1) the snout clears first; falling (e > 1) the snout goes back under first.
 */
export function emergedAt(e: number, s: number) {
  if (e <= 1) return smoothstep(0, 1, clamp((1.3 * e - s) / 0.3, 0, 1));
  return 1 - smoothstep(0, 1, clamp((1.3 * (e - 1) - s) / 0.3, 0, 1));
}

/** The depth a body point is seen at: the part out of the water has no water veil, so it blends toward 1. */
function emergedDepth(d0: number, e: number, s: number) {
  return e === 0 ? d0 : d0 + (1 - d0) * emergedAt(e, s);
}

/** Draw key: a breaching animal (height) draws over the others at the same depth. */
function depthKey(sh: Creature) {
  return sh.depth + 0.6 * sh.height;
}

/**
 * Creature indices in draw order, ascending by depthKey. An insertion sort into `out` (preallocated);
 * it is stable, so ties keep the array order, and without height the order is the array order.
 */
export function drawOrder(creatures: readonly Creature[], out: Int16Array) {
  for (let i = 0; i < creatures.length; i++) out[i] = i;
  for (let i = 1; i < creatures.length; i++) {
    const v = out[i];
    const key = depthKey(creatures[v]);
    let k = i;
    while (k > 0 && depthKey(creatures[out[k - 1]]) > key) {
      out[k] = out[k - 1];
      k--;
    }
    out[k] = v;
  }
}

/** Most eats per frame that stamp heat. */
const EAT_HEAT_PER_FRAME = 4;
/** Most bite ripple sets started per frame. */
const EAT_RIPPLE_SETS_PER_FRAME = 2;
/** Recent ripple starts remembered, so bites at one spot do not stack sets on top of each other. */
const RIPPLE_HISTORY = 8;
const RIPPLE_HISTORY_SECONDS = 0.3;
/** A splash has no body length; it pushes the pond plants as a koi-sized bite would. */
const SPLASH_PUSH_SIZE = 60;
/** A koi with dive below this swims shallow: it pushes the pond plants and shows on top of them. */
const SHALLOW_DIVE = 0.35;
/** A new set must start this many body lengths away from a recent one. */
const RIPPLE_SPACING = 0.25;

export class AsciiRenderer {
  private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D;
  private bg: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private dpr = 1;
  private width = 0;
  private height = 0;
  private cw = 12;
  private ch = 18;
  private cols = 0;
  private rows = 0;
  private fontFamily = "ui-monospace, Menlo, monospace";

  private dotKind = new Uint8Array(0); // 0 none, 1 blue, 2 green, 3 red, 4 pale
  private dotPhase = new Float32Array(0);
  private dotFreq = new Float32Array(0);
  private energy = new Float32Array(0);
  private energyTmp = new Float32Array(0);
  private heat = new Float32Array(0);
  private bucketOf = new Uint8Array(0);
  private bucketOrder = new Int32Array(0);
  private bucketCounts = new Int32Array(DOT_RGB.length * ALPHA_LEVELS + 1);
  /** drawDots scratch: each bucket's start in bucketOrder, and the fill cursor. */
  private bucketStarts = new Int32Array(DOT_RGB.length * ALPHA_LEVELS + 1);
  private bucketCursor = new Int32Array(DOT_RGB.length * ALPHA_LEVELS + 1);
  private dotStyles: string[] = [];
  /** Food glyph colours: [scene][level], scene 0 ocean and 1 pond. */
  private morselStyles: string[][] = [];
  /** Morsel cells drawn this frame (frame stamp); the sea skips them. */
  private morselStamp = new Uint32Array(0);
  /** Surface rings, spray, bubbles and mist: spawned by events, drawn in both colour modes. */
  private effects = new Effects();
  /** Ring starts of the last RIPPLE_HISTORY bite sets: x, y and world time. */
  private rippleX = new Float64Array(RIPPLE_HISTORY);
  private rippleY = new Float64Array(RIPPLE_HISTORY);
  private rippleT = new Float64Array(RIPPLE_HISTORY).fill(-Infinity);
  private rippleNext = 0;
  private ripplesThisFrame = 0;
  /** Ring colours by alpha level. */
  private ringStyles: string[] = [];
  /** Pond scene: lily pads and duckweed, and the cells the pads cover (rings skip them). */
  private pond = new PondSurface();
  private padMask = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Pond scene: cells held by a shallow koi, which the pond plants leave on top. */
  private koiMask = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** The pond plants need a koi length to lay out, so layout waits for the first render with a creature. */
  private pondPending = true;
  /** This frame's scene is the pond. Turns off the open sea. */
  private pondScene = false;
  /** Particle colours: [kind][level], kind 0 spray, 1 bubble, 2 blow mist. */
  private particleStyles: string[][] = [];
  /** The open sea: swell, glitter, windrows and whitecaps. Natural water with the surface on only. */
  private sea = new SeaSurface();
  private swellLit: string[] = [];
  private swellShade: string[] = [];
  private glintStyles: string[] = [];
  private streakStyles: string[] = [];
  private foamStyles: string[] = [];
  /** Natural colours only: the sea and the ripple sets; see setSurface. */
  private surface = true;
  /** Counts renders; mixed into effect seeds so each frame's randomness differs. */
  private frameCount = 0;
  // Particle draw passes, bound once so the per-frame forEach allocates nothing.
  private drawBubbleFn = (x: number, y: number, kind: ParticleKind, age01: number) => {
    if (kind === 1) this.drawParticle(x, y, kind, age01, true);
  };
  private drawAirFn = (x: number, y: number, kind: ParticleKind, age01: number) => {
    if (kind !== 1) this.drawParticle(x, y, kind, age01, false);
  };
  private drawRingFn = (
    x: number,
    y: number,
    r: number,
    alpha: number,
    seed: number,
    age: number,
  ) => {
    this.drawRing(x, y, r, alpha, seed, age);
  };

  private bodyFlag = new Uint8Array(0);
  private bodyShade = new Float32Array(0);
  private bodyKey = new Uint32Array(0);
  private bodyDepth = new Float32Array(0);
  private bodyKind = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Cells darkened by a creature's shadow on the seabed. */
  private bodyShadow = new Uint8Array(0);
  // Natural mode: each body cell has a top (2i) and bottom (2i + 1) half.
  private halfFlag = new Uint8Array(0);
  private halfShade = new Float32Array(0);
  /** Natural mode, coat species: the coat region of each half cell. */
  private halfRegion = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Natural mode: index into bodyCoat of the animal that last drew each cell. */
  private bodyCreature = new Uint8Array(0);
  /** Natural mode: this frame's coat per animal (null for species without one), indexed by bodyCreature. */
  private bodyCoat: (Coat | null)[] = [];
  /** Natural mode: how hard each cell's seabed shadow darkens the dots, 0 to 1. */
  private bodyShadowStrength = new Float32Array(0);
  /** Natural mode: quantised rgb (5 bits per channel) to its fillStyle string. */
  private styleCache: string[] = new Array(STYLE_CACHE_SIZE);
  private spine = new Float32Array(SPINE_NODES * 2);
  private arc = new Float32Array(SPINE_NODES);
  private arcTotal = 1;
  private def: CreatureDef = CREATURES.classic;
  private bodyId = 0;
  private finTipLeft = 0;
  private finTipRight = 0;
  /** Tail-fin beat for the current creature: lift is sin(phase), beat scales the flukes with it. */
  private tailLift = 0;
  private tailBeat = 1;
  // Scratch outputs of sampleBody(), to avoid allocating per sample.
  private hitFlag = 0;
  private hitShade = 0;
  private hitAcross = 0;
  private hitS = 0;
  private hitV = 0;
  private hitDist = 0;
  /** Reused for every coat paint call, so painting allocates nothing. belly and bellyAmount are set for every point. */
  private paintPoint: PaintPoint = {
    s: 0,
    v: 0,
    L: 0,
    across: 0,
    flag: 0,
    id: 0,
    belly: false,
    bellyAmount: 0,
  };
  /** Creature indices in draw order (see drawOrder); resized only when the creature count changes. */
  private order = new Int16Array(0);
  // Pose factors for the creature being rasterized. Neutral (1, false, 0) when its poses are at rest.
  /** Roll: width factor on the body and fins. */
  private wf = 1;
  /** Roll past a quarter turn: a pale belly. */
  private belly = false;
  /** How far the pale belly shows, 0–1; eased across the roll so it grows in rather than popping on. */
  private bellyAmount = 0;
  /** Gape: widens the head, and darkens the mouth when open. */
  private gape = 0;
  private mouthOpen = false;
  /** Height: lifts the body shade. */
  private lift = 0;
  /** Height: how far the shadow falls. */
  private shadowMul = 1;
  /** Height: how strong the natural shadow is. */
  private shadowFade = 1;
  /** Airborne: how much bigger the body looks from above, 1 at rest and through a breath (see setPoseFactors). */
  private airScale = 1;
  /** The creature's emerge (0 under, 1 out, 2 under) and its airborne amount a, for the head-first pose. */
  private emerge = 0;
  private emergeA = 0;

  /** Cells holding a fish this frame (frame stamp); dots skip them. */
  private fishStamp = new Uint32Array(0);
  private frameId = 0;
  /** Fish cells bucketed by brightness, so each bucket is one fillStyle. */
  private fishBuckets: number[][] = FISH_ALPHAS.map(() => []);
  /** Stunned fish cells this frame: drawn in the brightest fish style, with a `~`. */
  private fishStunned: number[] = [];
  private fishStyles: string[] = [];
  private palette: OceanPalette = DEFAULT_PALETTE;
  private colors: OceanColors = "theme";
  /** OKLab lightness of the page background, for the coat contrast floor. Set by setPalette. */
  private bgL = rgbToOklabL(...DEFAULT_PALETTE.background);
  /** Coat ramps for this page; rebuilt whenever the palette changes. */
  private rampCache = new WeakMap<Coat, Uint8Array[]>();
  /** The scene's water and seabed; see setScene. */
  private scene: OceanScene = "ocean";
  private water: WaterTables = OCEAN_WATER;
  /** Seed for the seabed's patches, rocks, pebbles and leaves. */
  private floorSeed = 0;

  constructor(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { alpha: false });
    if (!ctx) throw new Error("2D canvas unavailable");
    this.ctx = ctx;
    this.bg = document.createElement("canvas");
    this.buildStyles();
  }

  /** Swap in the page colours: rebuilds the ink styles and repaints the background. */
  setPalette(p: OceanPalette) {
    this.palette = p;
    this.bgL = rgbToOklabL(...p.background);
    this.rampCache = new WeakMap();
    this.buildStyles();
    this.styleCache = new Array(STYLE_CACHE_SIZE);
    if (this.bg.width > 0) this.paintBackground();
  }

  /** Natural keeps animals in their real colours; theme keeps them greyscale and inverts on light pages. */
  setColors(mode: OceanColors) {
    this.colors = mode;
    this.buildStyles();
    this.styleCache = new Array(STYLE_CACHE_SIZE);
    // The seabed is natural-only, so the background repaints when the mode flips.
    if (this.bg.width > 0) this.paintBackground();
  }

  /**
   * The scene sets the water (pond green-brown, ocean blue) and the seabed under the grid.
   * `seed` picks the seabed's patches, rocks and pebbles, so one seed gives one floor.
   */
  setScene(scene: OceanScene, seed = 0) {
    this.scene = scene;
    this.water = scene === "pond" ? POND_WATER : OCEAN_WATER;
    this.floorSeed = seed;
    // Cached styles hold water-attenuated colours, so they belong to the old water.
    this.rampCache = new WeakMap();
    this.styleCache = new Array(STYLE_CACHE_SIZE);
    if (this.bg.width > 0) this.paintBackground();
  }

  /** The open sea and the surface rings (natural colours only). Turn off for a calmer background. */
  setSurface(on: boolean) {
    this.surface = on;
  }

  private buildStyles() {
    const { foreground, mode } = this.palette;
    this.fishStyles = FISH_ALPHAS.map((a) => `rgba(${foreground.join(",")},${a})`);
    const levelStyles = (rgb: readonly number[], peak: number) =>
      Array.from(
        { length: ALPHA_LEVELS },
        (_, k) => `rgba(${rgb.join(",")},${((peak * (k + 1)) / ALPHA_LEVELS).toFixed(3)})`,
      );
    this.ringStyles = levelStyles(mode === "light" ? WAKE_RGB_LIGHT : DOT_RGB[WAKE], 1);
    const spray = levelStyles(mode === "light" ? SPRAY_RGB.light : SPRAY_RGB.dark, PARTICLE_ALPHA);
    const bubble = levelStyles(
      mode === "light" ? BUBBLE_RGB.light : BUBBLE_RGB.dark,
      PARTICLE_ALPHA,
    );
    const mist = levelStyles(mode === "light" ? SPRAY_RGB.light : SPRAY_RGB.dark, MIST_ALPHA);
    this.particleStyles = [spray, bubble, mist];
    this.swellLit = rampStyles(SWELL_RGB[mode], SWELL_PEAK[mode].lit, SWELL_LEVELS);
    this.swellShade = rampStyles(SWELL_SHADE_RGB, SWELL_PEAK[mode].shade, SWELL_LEVELS);
    this.glintStyles = GLINT_ALPHA[mode].map((a) => `rgba(${GLINT_RGB[mode].join(",")},${a})`);
    this.streakStyles = STREAK_ALPHA.map((a) => `rgba(${FOAM_RGB[mode].join(",")},${a})`);
    this.foamStyles = FOAM_ALPHA.map((a) => `rgba(${FOAM_RGB[mode].join(",")},${a})`);
    this.dotStyles = [];
    for (let c = 0; c < DOT_RGB.length; c++) {
      const [r, g, b] = c === WAKE && mode === "light" ? WAKE_RGB_LIGHT : DOT_RGB[c];
      for (let l = 0; l < ALPHA_LEVELS; l++) {
        const a = (DOT_BASE_ALPHA[c] * (l + 1)) / ALPHA_LEVELS;
        this.dotStyles.push(`rgba(${r},${g},${b},${a.toFixed(3)})`);
      }
    }
    this.morselStyles = MORSEL_RGB[mode].map(([r, g, b]) =>
      Array.from(
        { length: MORSEL_LEVELS },
        (_, k) => `rgba(${r},${g},${b},${((MORSEL_ALPHA * (k + 1)) / MORSEL_LEVELS).toFixed(3)})`,
      ),
    );
    this.pond.setPalette(mode);
  }

  /** Greys invert on light backgrounds, so the creatures read as a negative. */
  private tone(level: number) {
    return this.palette.mode === "dark" ? level : 255 - level;
  }

  get cellWidth() {
    return this.cw;
  }

  get cellHeight() {
    return this.ch;
  }

  setFontFamily(family: string) {
    if (family) this.fontFamily = family;
    // The natural floor is drawn in this font, so a sized canvas repaints it.
    if (family && this.bg.width > 0) this.paintBackground();
  }

  resize(width: number, height: number, dpr: number) {
    this.width = width;
    this.height = height;
    this.dpr = dpr;
    this.cw = clamp(Math.round(width / 124), 8, 13);
    this.ch = Math.round(this.cw * 1.45);
    this.cols = Math.ceil(width / this.cw);
    this.rows = Math.ceil(height / this.ch);

    this.canvas.width = Math.round(width * dpr);
    this.canvas.height = Math.round(height * dpr);
    this.bg.width = this.canvas.width;
    this.bg.height = this.canvas.height;

    const n = this.cols * this.rows;
    this.dotKind = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.dotPhase = new Float32Array(n);
    this.dotFreq = new Float32Array(n);
    this.energy = new Float32Array(n);
    this.energyTmp = new Float32Array(n);
    this.heat = new Float32Array(n);
    this.bucketOf = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.bucketOrder = new Int32Array(n);
    this.bodyFlag = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.bodyShade = new Float32Array(n);
    this.bodyKey = new Uint32Array(n);
    this.bodyDepth = new Float32Array(n);
    this.bodyKind = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.bodyShadow = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.halfFlag = new Uint8Array(2 * n);
    this.halfShade = new Float32Array(2 * n);
    this.halfRegion = new Uint8Array(2 * n);
    this.bodyCreature = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.bodyShadowStrength = new Float32Array(n);
    this.fishStamp = new Uint32Array(n);
    this.morselStamp = new Uint32Array(n);
    this.sea.layout(this.cols, this.rows, this.cw, this.ch, SEA_SEED);
    this.padMask = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.koiMask = new Uint8Array(n);
    this.pondPending = true;

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const col = i % this.cols;
      const row = (i / this.cols) | 0;
      // No ambient dots: the grid stays black. Only the cyan wake lights cells.
      this.dotKind[i] = 0;
      this.dotPhase[i] = hash3(col, row, 23) * Math.PI * 2;
      this.dotFreq[i] = 0.3 + hash3(col, row, 37) * 1.1;
    }
    this.paintBackground();
  }

  private paintBackground() {
    const g = this.bg.getContext("2d");
    if (!g) return;
    const { width: W, height: H, dpr, cw, ch } = this;
    const { background, foreground, mode } = this.palette;
    g.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    g.fillStyle = `rgb(${background.join(",")})`;
    g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    // Natural colours have a seabed under the grid; theme keeps the flat background.
    if (this.colors === "natural") this.paintFloor(g);
    paintGrid(
      g,
      W,
      H,
      cw,
      ch,
      dpr,
      `rgba(${foreground.join(",")},${mode === "dark" ? 0.055 : 0.07})`,
    );
  }

  /**
   * The seabed, painted once into the background. Each cell gets at most one glyph, and the patches
   * are faint fills under them. Every colour is seen through the scene's water at the far depth.
   */
  private paintFloor(g: CanvasRenderingContext2D) {
    const { cols, rows, cw, ch, scene, floorSeed, fontFamily } = this;
    const bg = this.palette.background;
    const dark = this.palette.mode === "dark";
    const z = (k: number) => FLOOR_SALT + floorSeed * 97 + k * 1009;
    const styles = new Map<string, string>();
    /** An OKLCH colour at an alpha, through the water and then veiled toward the page. Memoised. */
    const fill = (c: Oklch, alpha: number) => {
      const key = `${c.join()}|${alpha}`;
      let s = styles.get(key);
      if (s === undefined) {
        const [r, gn, b] = attenuate(oklchToRgb(c[0], c[1], c[2]), bg, FLOOR_DEPTH, scene);
        const veiled = (x: number, i: number) => Math.round(x + (bg[i] - x) * FLOOR_VEIL);
        s = `rgba(${veiled(r, 0)},${veiled(gn, 1)},${veiled(b, 2)},${alpha.toFixed(3)})`;
        styles.set(key, s);
      }
      return s;
    };

    const taken = new Uint8Array(cols * rows);
    const free = (col: number, row: number) =>
      col >= 0 && row >= 0 && col < cols && row < rows && !taken[row * cols + col];
    const glyph = (text: string, col: number, row: number, style: string) => {
      g.fillStyle = style;
      g.fillText(text, (col + 0.5) * cw, row * ch + ch / 2 + 1);
      taken[row * cols + col] = 1;
    };
    g.font = `600 ${Math.round(ch * 0.74)}px ${fontFamily}`;
    g.textAlign = "center";
    g.textBaseline = "middle";

    if (scene === "ocean") {
      // Rocks: 3–6 clusters of 3–9 cells. The centre is the darkest cell and gets the '@'.
      const clusters = 3 + Math.floor(hash3(0, 0, z(1)) * 4);
      for (let k = 0; k < clusters; k++) {
        const n = 3 + Math.floor(hash3(k, 3, z(1)) * 7);
        const cx = Math.floor(hash3(k, 1, z(1)) * cols);
        const cy = Math.floor(hash3(k, 2, z(1)) * rows);
        // Candidates in a 5×3 box, nearest first. The hash breaks ties, so clusters differ in shape.
        const cand: [number, number, number][] = [];
        for (let dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++) {
          for (let dc = -2; dc <= 2; dc++) {
            cand.push([dc, dr, dc * dc + 4 * dr * dr + 0.9 * hash3(cx + dc, cy + dr, z(10 + k))]);
          }
        }
        cand.sort((a, b) => a[2] - b[2]);
        for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
          const col = cx + cand[j][0];
          const row = cy + cand[j][1];
          if (!free(col, row)) continue;
          if (j === 0) glyph("@", col, row, fill(ROCK, 0.32));
          else glyph(hash3(col, row, z(20)) < 0.5 ? "O" : "o", col, row, fill(ROCK, 0.2));
        }
      }

      // Sand ripples: long slanted ridges. Alternate cells along a ridge draw '-', so it reads as sand, not a solid stripe.
      const cosT = Math.cos(RIPPLE_THETA);
      const sinT = Math.sin(RIPPLE_THETA);
      const base = dark ? 0.22 : 0.16;
      for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
        for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
          if (!free(col, row)) continue;
          const v = Math.sin(
            (TAU * (col * cosT + row * sinT)) / RIPPLE_LAMBDA +
              0.9 * Math.sin((TAU * row) / (5 * RIPPLE_LAMBDA)),
          );
          if (v <= RIPPLE_RIDGE) continue;
          // The patch factor (0.6–1) fades the ripples in and out across the floor, in 0.1 steps.
          const patch = Math.round((0.6 + 0.4 * floorNoise(col, row, cols, rows, z(30))) * 10) / 10;
          glyph(col % 2 ? "-" : "~", col, row, fill(SAND, base * patch));
        }
      }

      // Shells: a few scattered dots and commas, faint.
      for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
        for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
          if (!free(col, row) || hash3(col, row, z(40)) >= 0.004) continue;
          glyph(hash3(col, row, z(41)) < 0.5 ? "." : ",", col, row, fill(SHELL, 0.2));
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Silt: a lower-frequency darker bed, as faint fills under the glyphs.
      for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
        for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
          const silt = floorNoise(col, row, cols, rows, z(50));
          if (silt <= 0.5) continue;
          g.fillStyle = fill(SILT, Math.round((0.08 + 0.12 * (silt - 0.5)) * 100) / 100);
          g.fillRect(col * cw, row * ch, cw, ch);
        }
      }

      // Sunken leaves: 4–8 two-cell pairs of brown '~~'.
      const leaves = 4 + Math.floor(hash3(0, 0, z(60)) * 5);
      for (let k = 0; k < leaves; k++) {
        const col = Math.floor(hash3(k, 1, z(61)) * cols);
        const row = Math.floor(hash3(k, 2, z(61)) * rows);
        if (!free(col, row) || !free(col + 1, row)) continue;
        glyph("~", col, row, fill(LEAF, 0.28));
        glyph("~", col + 1, row, fill(LEAF, 0.28));
      }

      // Pebbles: 5% of cells, rounded and mixed in tone, alpha 0.3–0.42.
      for (let row = 0; row < rows; row++) {
        for (let col = 0; col < cols; col++) {
          if (!free(col, row) || hash3(col, row, z(70)) >= 0.05) continue;
          const tone = PEBBLES[Math.floor(hash3(col, row, z(71)) * PEBBLES.length)];
          const alpha = Math.round((0.3 + 0.12 * hash3(col, row, z(72))) * 50) / 50;
          const shape = ["o", "°", "."][Math.floor(hash3(col, row, z(73)) * 3)];
          glyph(shape, col, row, fill(tone, alpha));
        }
      }
    }
  }

  render(world: World, dt: number) {
    const { ctx, dpr, cols, ch } = this;
    if (cols === 0) return;
    const t = world.time;
    const natural = this.colors === "natural";
    const surface = natural && this.surface;
    // A still pond has no open sea. Its plants float on natural, surface-on water only.
    this.pondScene = world.scene === "pond";
    const plants = surface && this.pondScene;
    const waves = surface && !this.pondScene;
    this.frameCount++;
    this.padMask.fill(0);
    if (plants && this.pondPending) {
      let bodyL = 0;
      for (const sh of world.creatures) bodyL = Math.max(bodyL, sh.length);
      if (bodyL > 0) {
        this.pond.layout(this.width, this.height, bodyL);
        this.pondPending = false;
      }
    }

    this.consumeEvents(world);
    this.effects.step(dt);
    if (waves) this.sea.step(t, dt);
    if (plants) {
      this.pondWake(world);
      this.pond.step(dt, t);
    }
    this.rasterizeCreatures(world.creatures, t);
    this.depositWake(world);
    this.diffuse(dt);

    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    ctx.drawImage(this.bg, 0, 0);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);

    this.markFish(world);
    this.drawDots(t);

    ctx.font = `600 ${Math.round(ch * 0.74)}px ${this.fontFamily}`;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";

    this.drawHeat();
    this.drawFish();
    this.drawMorsels(world);
    if (waves) this.drawSea(t);
    this.effects.forEachParticle(this.drawBubbleFn);
    // Pad shadows fall on the floor, so the creatures swim over them.
    if (plants) this.pond.drawShadows(ctx, this.cw, ch, cols, this.rows);
    if (natural) this.drawNatural(t);
    else this.drawCreatures(t);
    if (plants) {
      this.markShallowKoi(world);
      this.pond.drawPlants(ctx, this.cw, ch, cols, this.rows, this.padMask, this.koiMask);
    }
    // Rings and spray sit above the water line, over the animals.
    this.effects.forEachRing(this.drawRingFn);
    this.effects.forEachParticle(this.drawAirFn);
  }

  // ── simulation → field ──────────────────────────────────────────────
```
